@@patch alg @sec-FinishLoadingImportedModule root/0:then:0..2
@@spectec
| let values = @ph-contains-such-binds(data choice 0, data sort #"LoadedModuleRequest Record", ?#"record", closure 0, referrer[[LoadedModules]])
| if $ListLen(values) == 1 or $ListLen(values) != 0 and expr $""() {
|       let #"record" = values[0]
|       assert expr @ph-same-tn(#"record"[[Module]], result[[Value]], data sort #"Module Record")
|     }
|     else {
|       perform $Append(record #"LoadedModuleRequest Record" { [[Specifier]]: moduleRequest[[Specifier]], [[Attributes]]: moduleRequest[[Attributes]], [[Module]]: result[[Value]] }, referrer[[LoadedModules]])
|     }
@@html
|   1. If _referrer_.[[LoadedModules]] contains a LoadedModuleRequest Record _record_ such that ModuleRequestsEqual(_record_, _moduleRequest_) is *true*, then
|     1. Assert: _record_.[[Module]] and _result_.[[Value]] are the same Module Record.
@@end

@@patch alg @sec-GetImportedModule root:0..3
@@spectec
|   let records = $ListNew()
|   for_each r : #"LoadedModuleRequest Record" in referrer[[LoadedModules]] {
|     if @sec-ModuleRequestsEqual(r, request) is val {true} -> perform $Append(?r, ?records)
|   }
|   assert $ListLen(?records) is val {1}
@@html
| 1. [declared="r"] Let _records_ be a List consisting of each LoadedModuleRequest Record _r_ of _referrer_.[[LoadedModules]] such that ModuleRequestsEqual(_r_, _request_) is *true*.
| 1. Assert: _records_ has exactly one element, since LoadRequestedModules has completed successfully on _referrer_ prior to invoking this abstract operation.
@@end

@@end

@@patch alg @sec-InnerModuleLinking root:8..9
@@spectec
|   for_each request : #"ModuleRequest Record" in module[[RequestedModules]] {
|     let requiredModule = @sec-GetImportedModule(module, request)
|     set var index = ? @sec-InnerModuleLinking(requiredModule, stack, index)
|     if requiredModule is ty {#"Cyclic Module Record"} {
|       assert requiredModule[[Status]] is val {~linking~; ~linked~; ~evaluating-async~; ~evaluated~}
|       assert expr @ph-iff(bool(requiredModule[[Status]] is val {~linking~}), bool(stack sat $Contains(?requiredModule)))
|       if requiredModule[[Status]] is val {~linking~} {
|         set field module[[DFSAncestorIndex]] = $min(module[[DFSAncestorIndex]], requiredModule[[DFSAncestorIndex]])
|       }
|     }
|   }
@@html
| 1. For each ModuleRequest Record _request_ of _module_.[[RequestedModules]], do
|   1. Let _requiredModule_ be GetImportedModule(_module_, _request_).
|   1. Set _index_ to ? InnerModuleLinking(_requiredModule_, _stack_, _index_).
|   1. If _requiredModule_ is a Cyclic Module Record, then
|     1. Assert: _requiredModule_.[[Status]] is one of ~linking~, ~linked~, ~evaluating-async~, or ~evaluated~.
|     1. Assert: _requiredModule_.[[Status]] is ~linking~ if and only if _stack_ contains _requiredModule_.
|     1. If _requiredModule_.[[Status]] is ~linking~, then
|       1. Set _module_.[[DFSAncestorIndex]] to min(_module_.[[DFSAncestorIndex]], _requiredModule_.[[DFSAncestorIndex]]).
@@end

@@patch alg @sec-InnerModuleLoading root/4:then:1..2
@@spectec
|     for_each loaded : #"Cyclic Module Record" in state[[Visited]] {
|       if loaded[[Status]] is val {~new~} -> set field loaded[[Status]] = ~unlinked~
|     }
@@html
|   1. For each Cyclic Module Record _loaded_ of _state_.[[Visited]], do
|     1. If _loaded_.[[Status]] is ~new~, set _loaded_.[[Status]] to ~unlinked~.
@@end

@@patch alg @sec-InnerModuleLoading root:1..2
@@spectec
|   if module is ty {#"Cyclic Module Record"} and module[[Status]] is val {~new~} and state[[Visited]] fails $Contains(?module) {
|     perform $Append(?module, state[[Visited]])
|     let requestedModulesCount = $ListLen(module[[RequestedModules]])
|     set field state[[PendingModulesCount]] = state[[PendingModulesCount]] + requestedModulesCount
|     for_each request : #"ModuleRequest Record" in module[[RequestedModules]] {
|       if @sec-AllImportAttributesSupported(request[[Attributes]]) is val {false} {
|         let error = @sec-throwcompletion(@ph-new-error(err [[SyntaxError]]))
|         perform @sec-ContinueModuleLoading(state, error)
|       }
|       else {
|         let values = @ph-contains-such-binds(data choice 0, data sort #"LoadedModuleRequest Record", ?#"record", closure 0, module[[LoadedModules]])
|         if $ListLen(values) == 1 or $ListLen(values) != 0 and expr $""() {
|           let #"record" = values[0]
|           perform @sec-InnerModuleLoading(state, #"record"[[Module]])
|         }
|         else {
|           perform @sec-HostLoadImportedModule(module, request, state[[HostDefined]], state)
|           note "HostLoadImportedModule will call FinishLoadingImportedModule, which re-enters the graph loading process through ContinueModuleLoading."
|         }
|       }
|       if state[[IsLoading]] is val {false} -> return ~unused~
|     }
|   }
@@html
| 1. If _module_ is a Cyclic Module Record, _module_.[[Status]] is ~new~, and _state_.[[Visited]] does not contain _module_, then
|   1. Append _module_ to _state_.[[Visited]].
|   1. Let _requestedModulesCount_ be the number of elements in _module_.[[RequestedModules]].
|   1. Set _state_.[[PendingModulesCount]] to _state_.[[PendingModulesCount]] + _requestedModulesCount_.
|   1. For each ModuleRequest Record _request_ of _module_.[[RequestedModules]], do
|     1. If AllImportAttributesSupported(_request_.[[Attributes]]) is *false*, then
|       1. Let _error_ be ThrowCompletion(a newly created *SyntaxError* object).
|       1. Perform ContinueModuleLoading(_state_, _error_).
|     1. Else if _module_.[[LoadedModules]] contains a LoadedModuleRequest Record _record_ such that ModuleRequestsEqual(_record_, _request_) is *true*, then
|       1. Perform InnerModuleLoading(_state_, _record_.[[Module]]).
|     1. Else,
|       1. Perform HostLoadImportedModule(_module_, _request_, _state_.[[HostDefined]], _state_).
|       1. NOTE: HostLoadImportedModule will call FinishLoadingImportedModule, which re-enters the graph loading process through ContinueModuleLoading.
|     1. If _state_.[[IsLoading]] is *false*, return ~unused~.
@@end

@@patch alg @sec-ModuleRequestsEqual root:6..7
@@spectec
|   for_each xAttr : #"ImportAttribute Record" in xAttrs {
|     if yAttrs sat @ph-not-contains-such(data choice 1, data sort #"ImportAttribute Record", ?yAttr, closure 0) -> return false
|   }
@@html
| 1. For each ImportAttribute Record _xAttr_ of _xAttrs_, do
|   1. If _yAttrs_ does not contain an ImportAttribute Record _yAttr_ such that _xAttr_.[[Key]] is _yAttr_.[[Key]] and _xAttr_.[[Value]] is _yAttr_.[[Value]], return *false*.
@@end

@@patch alg @sec-ParseJSON root:7..9
@@spectec

|     assert expr @ph-contains-cond(?script, |ArrayLiteral|) or expr @ph-contains-cond(?script, |ObjectLiteral|)
|   }
|   else {
|     assert result is ty {String; Number; Boolean} or result is val {null}
|   }
@@html

@@end

@@patch alg @sec-async-module-execution-fulfilled root:11..12
@@spectec
|   for_each ancestorModule : #"Cyclic Module Record" in sortedExecList {
|     if ancestorModule[[Status]] is val {~evaluated~} {
|       assert ancestorModule[[EvaluationError]] is not val {~empty~}
|     }
|     else_if ancestorModule[[HasTLA]] is val {true} {
|       perform @sec-execute-async-module(ancestorModule)
|     }
|     else {
|       let result = @sec-completion-ao(ancestorModule.ExecuteModule())
|       if result is ty {#"abrupt completion"} {
|         perform @sec-async-module-execution-rejected(ancestorModule, result[[Value]])
|       }
|       else {
|         set field ancestorModule[[AsyncEvaluationOrder]] = ~done~
|         set field ancestorModule[[Status]] = ~evaluated~
|         if ancestorModule[[TopLevelCapability]] is not val {~empty~} {
|           assert expr @ph-same-tn(ancestorModule[[CycleRoot]], ?ancestorModule, data sort #"Module Record")
|           perform ! @sec-call(ancestorModule[[TopLevelCapability]][[Resolve]], undefined, [undefined])
|         }
|       }
|     }
|   }
@@html
| 1. For each Cyclic Module Record _ancestorModule_ of _sortedExecList_, do
|   1. If _ancestorModule_.[[Status]] is ~evaluated~, then
|     1. Assert: _ancestorModule_.[[EvaluationError]] is not ~empty~.
|   1. Else if _ancestorModule_.[[HasTLA]] is *true*, then
|     1. Perform ExecuteAsyncModule(_ancestorModule_).
|   1. Else,
|     1. Let _result_ be Completion(<emu-meta effects="user-code">_ancestorModule_.ExecuteModule()</emu-meta>).
|     1. If _result_ is an abrupt completion, then
|       1. Perform AsyncModuleExecutionRejected(_ancestorModule_, _result_.[[Value]]).
|     1. Else,
|       1. Set _ancestorModule_.[[AsyncEvaluationOrder]] to ~done~.
|       1. Set _ancestorModule_.[[Status]] to ~evaluated~.
|       1. If _ancestorModule_.[[TopLevelCapability]] is not ~empty~, then
|         1. Assert: _ancestorModule_.[[CycleRoot]] and _ancestorModule_ are the same Module Record.
|         1. Perform ! Call(_ancestorModule_.[[TopLevelCapability]].[[Resolve]], *undefined*, « *undefined* »).
@@end

@@patch alg @sec-async-module-execution-rejected root:9..10
@@spectec
|   for_each ancestorModule : #"Cyclic Module Record" in module[[AsyncParentModules]] {
|     perform @sec-async-module-execution-rejected(ancestorModule, error)
|   }
@@html
| 1. For each Cyclic Module Record _ancestorModule_ of _module_.[[AsyncParentModules]], do
|   1. Perform AsyncModuleExecutionRejected(_ancestorModule_, _error_).
@@end

@@patch alg @sec-bigint.asintn root:2..8
@@spectec
|   if bits == 0 or bigint is val {num_z(0)} -> return num_z(0)
|   let bound = 4 * ($DecimalExponent($abs($ℝ(bigint))) + 1)
|   if bits - 1 >= bound -> return bigint
|   let mod = $ℝ(bigint) modulo 2 ^ bits
|   if mod >= 2 ^ {bits - 1} -> return $ℤ(mod - 2 ^ bits)
|   return $ℤ(mod)
@@html
| 1. Let _remainder_ be ℝ(_bigint_) modulo 2<sup>_bits_</sup>.
| 1. If _remainder_ ≥ 2<sup>_bits_ - 1</sup>, return ℤ(_remainder_ - 2<sup>_bits_</sup>).
| 1. Return ℤ(_remainder_).
@@end

@@end

@@patch alg @sec-coherent-reads root:0..1
@@spectec
|   for_each readEvent : ReadSharedMemory | ReadModifyWriteSharedMemory in @sec-sharedatablockeventset(execution) {
|     let writes = $""([readEvent], ?execution)
|     let byteLocation = readEvent[[ByteIndex]]
|     for_each writeEvent in writes {
|       if expr $""(?readEvent, data choice 0, ?writeEvent, ?execution) {
|         return false
|       }
|       if pass "there exists a WriteSharedMemory or ReadModifyWriteSharedMemory event _ that has _ in its memory range such that _ happens-before _ in _ and _ happens-before _ in _" {
|         return false
|       }
|       set var byteLocation = byteLocation + 1
|     }
|   }
@@html

|   1. Let _byteLocation_ be _readEvent_.[[ByteIndex]].
|   1. For each element _writeEvent_ of _writes_, do
|     1. If _readEvent_ happens-before _writeEvent_ in _execution_, then
|       1. Return *false*.
|     1. If there exists a WriteSharedMemory or ReadModifyWriteSharedMemory event _value_ that has _byteLocation_ in its memory range such that _writeEvent_ happens-before _value_ in _execution_ and _value_ happens-before _readEvent_ in _execution_, then
|       1. Return *false*.

@@end

@@patch alg @sec-createintrinsics root:0..5
@@spectec
|   set field realmRecord[[Intrinsics]] = $NewRecord(data sort Record)
|   let entries = $IntrinsicObjects()
|   let objects = $ListNew()
|   for_each entry : Record in entries {
|     if_else entry[[Prototype]] is val {null} { let proto = null } else { let proto = objects[entry[[Prototype]]] }
|     if entry[[Behaviour]] is not val {~empty~} {
|       if_else entry[[Prefix]] is val {~empty~} { let #"obj" = @sec-createbuiltinfunction(entry[[Behaviour]], entry[[Length]], entry[[Name]], entry[[Slots]], realmRecord, proto) } else { let #"obj" = @sec-createbuiltinfunction(entry[[Behaviour]], entry[[Length]], entry[[Name]], entry[[Slots]], realmRecord, proto, entry[[Prefix]]) }
|       set field #"obj"[[Async]] = entry[[Async]]
|       if entry[[Constructor]] is val {true} -> set field #"obj"[[Construct]] = ref @sec-built-in-function-objects-construct-argumentslist-newtarget
|     }
|     else {
|       let #"obj" = @sec-ordinaryobjectcreate(proto, entry[[Slots]])
|       if entry[[Exotic]] is val {clause @sec-array-exotic-objects} {
|         set field #"obj"[[DefineOwnProperty]] = ref @sec-array-exotic-objects-defineownproperty-p-desc
|       }
|       else_if entry[[Exotic]] is val {clause @sec-string-exotic-objects} {
|         set field #"obj"[[GetOwnProperty]] = ref @sec-string-exotic-objects-getownproperty-p
|         set field #"obj"[[DefineOwnProperty]] = ref @sec-string-exotic-objects-defineownproperty-p-desc
|         set field #"obj"[[OwnPropertyKeys]] = ref @sec-string-exotic-objects-ownpropertykeys
|       }
|       else_if entry[[Exotic]] is val {clause @sec-immutable-prototype-exotic-objects} {
|         set field #"obj"[[SetPrototypeOf]] = ref @sec-immutable-prototype-exotic-objects-setprototypeof-v
|       }
|     }
|     for_each #"slot" : Record in entry[[SlotValues]] {
|       perform $SetFieldNamed(#"obj", #"slot"[[Name]], #"slot"[[Value]])
|     }
|     for_each name in entry[[Names]] {
|       perform $SetFieldNamed(realmRecord[[Intrinsics]], name, #"obj")
|     }
|     perform $Append(?#"obj", ?objects)
|   }
|   for_each entry : Record in entries {
|     let #"obj" = objects[entry[[Index]]]
|     if entry[[Behaviour]] is not val {~empty~} {
|       let la = entry[[LengthAttributes]]
|       perform ! @sec-definepropertyorthrow(#"obj", "length", record PropertyDescriptor { [[Writable]]: la[[Writable]], [[Enumerable]]: la[[Enumerable]], [[Configurable]]: la[[Configurable]] })
|       let na = entry[[NameAttributes]]
|       perform ! @sec-definepropertyorthrow(#"obj", "name", record PropertyDescriptor { [[Writable]]: na[[Writable]], [[Enumerable]]: na[[Enumerable]], [[Configurable]]: na[[Configurable]] })
|     }
|     for_each p : Record in $PropertyOrder(#"obj", entry[[Properties]]) {
|       if @formal-propertyprovided(p) is val {true} -> perform $OwnPropertyCreate(#"obj", p[[Key]], @formal-intrinsicpropertydescriptor(p, objects), p[[Kind]])
|     }
|     set field #"obj"[[Extensible]] = entry[[Extensible]]
|   }
@@html
| 1. Set _realmRecord_.[[Intrinsics]] to a new Record.
| 1. [declared="steps,name,length,slots,proto,async"] Set fields of _realmRecord_.[[Intrinsics]] with the values listed in <emu-xref href="#table-well-known-intrinsic-objects"></emu-xref>. The field names are the names listed in the “Intrinsic Name” column of the table. The value of each field is a new object value fully and recursively populated with property values as defined by the specification of each object in clauses <emu-xref href="#sec-global-object"></emu-xref> through <emu-xref href="#sec-reflection"></emu-xref>. All object property values are newly created object values. All values that are built-in function objects are created by performing CreateBuiltinFunction(_steps_, _length_, _name_, _slots_, _realmRecord_, _proto_, _async_) where _steps_ is the definition of that function provided by this specification, _name_ is the initial value of the function's *"name"* property, _length_ is the initial value of the function's *"length"* property, _slots_ is a list of the names, if any, of the function's specified internal slots, _proto_ is the specified value of the function's [[Prototype]] internal slot, and _async_ is *true* if the function is described as “async” and *false* otherwise. The creation of the intrinsics and their properties must be ordered to avoid any dependencies upon objects that have not yet been created.
@@end

@@patch alg @sec-createjsonparserecord root/4:then/1:else:3..5
@@spectec
|       let #"keys" = ! @sec-enumerableownproperties(value, ~key~)
|       for_each propertyKey : String in #"keys" {
|         note "In the case of JSON text specifying multiple name/value pairs with the same name for a single object (such as <code>{\"a\":\"lost\",\"a\":\"kept\"}</code>), the value for the corresponding property of the resulting ECMAScript object is specified by the last pair with that name."
|         let propertyDefinition = ~empty~
|         for_each propertyNode : #"Parse Node" in propertyNodes {
|           let propertyName = sdo PropName(propertyNode)
|           if propertyName is val {propertyKey} -> set var propertyDefinition = propertyNode
|         }
|         assert propertyDefinition is val {prods [p"Syntactic - PropertyDefinition : PropertyName[?Yield, ?Await] `:` AssignmentExpression[+In, ?Yield, ?Await]"]}
|         let propertyValueNode = $ChildOf(|AssignmentExpression|, ?propertyDefinition)
|         let entryParseRecord = @sec-createjsonparserecord(propertyValueNode, propertyKey, ! @sec-get-o-p(value, propertyKey))
|         perform $Append(?entryParseRecord, ?entries)
|       }
@@html
|     1. Let _keys_ be ! <emu-meta suppress-effects="user-code">EnumerableOwnProperties(_value_, ~key~)</emu-meta>.
|     1. For each String _propertyKey_ of _keys_, do
|       1. NOTE: In the case of JSON text specifying multiple name/value pairs with the same name for a single object (such as <code>{"a":"lost","a":"kept"}</code>), the value for the corresponding property of the resulting ECMAScript object is specified by the last pair with that name.
|       1. Let _propertyDefinition_ be ~empty~.
|       1. For each Parse Node _propertyNode_ of _propertyNodes_, do
|         1. Let _propertyName_ be the PropName of _propertyNode_.
|         1. If _propertyName_ is _propertyKey_, set _propertyDefinition_ to _propertyNode_.
|       1. Assert: _propertyDefinition_ is <emu-grammar>PropertyDefinition : PropertyName `:` AssignmentExpression</emu-grammar>.
|       1. Let _propertyValueNode_ be the |AssignmentExpression| of _propertyDefinition_.
|       1. Let _entryParseRecord_ be CreateJSONParseRecord(_propertyValueNode_, _propertyKey_, ! <emu-meta suppress-effects="user-code">Get(_value_, _propertyKey_)</emu-meta>).
|       1. Append _entryParseRecord_ to _entries_.
@@end

@@patch alg @sec-encodeforregexpescape root:4..5
@@spectec
|   if toEscape sat $Contains(?codePoint) or codePoint sat @ph-matched-by-either([|WhiteSpace|, |LineTerminator|]) or codePoint sat @ph-surrogate-value() {
|     let codePointNumber = $NumericValue(?codePoint)
|     if codePointNumber <= math(hex(255, "FF")) {
|       let #"hex" = @sec-numeric-types-number-tostring($𝔽(codePointNumber), 16)
|       return $"string-concatenation"($CodeUnit(cu(92, "005C", "REVERSE SOLIDUS")), "x", @sec-stringpad(#"hex", 2, "0", ~start~))
|     }
|     let escaped = $"the empty String"()
|     let codeUnits = @sec-utf16encodecodepoint(codePoint)
|     for_range i incl 0 .. excl $StrLen(?codeUnits) {
|       let codeUnit = $UnitAt(?i, ?codeUnits)
|       set var escaped = $"string-concatenation"(escaped, @sec-unicodeescape(codeUnit))
|     }
|     return escaped
|   }
@@html
| 1. If _toEscape_ contains _codePoint_, _codePoint_ is matched by either |WhiteSpace| or |LineTerminator|, or _codePoint_ has the same numeric value as a leading surrogate or trailing surrogate, then
|   1. Let _codePointNumber_ be the numeric value of _codePoint_.
|   1. If _codePointNumber_ ≤ 0xFF, then
|     1. Let _hex_ be Number::toString(𝔽(_codePointNumber_), 16).
|     1. Return the string-concatenation of the code unit 0x005C (REVERSE SOLIDUS), *"x"*, and StringPad(_hex_, 2, *"0"*, ~start~).
|   1. Let _escaped_ be the empty String.
|   1. Let _codeUnits_ be UTF16EncodeCodePoint(_codePoint_).
|   1. For each code unit _codeUnit_ of _codeUnits_, do
|     1. Set _escaped_ to the string-concatenation of _escaped_ and UnicodeEscape(_codeUnit_).
|   1. Return _escaped_.
@@end

@@patch alg @sec-escaperegexppattern root:3..4
@@spectec
|   let escapedPattern = $EscapePattern(pattern, flags)
@@html
| 1. Let _escapedPattern_ be a String in the form of a _patternSymbol_ equivalent to _pattern_ interpreted as UTF-16 encoded Unicode code points (<emu-xref href="#sec-ecmascript-language-types-string-type"></emu-xref>), in which certain code points are escaped as described below. _escapedPattern_ may or may not differ from _pattern_; however, the Abstract Closure that would result from evaluating _escapedPattern_ as a _patternSymbol_ must behave identically to the Abstract Closure given by the constructed object's [[RegExpMatcher]] internal slot. Multiple calls to this abstract operation using the same values for _pattern_ and _flags_ must produce identical results.
| 1. The code points `/` or any |LineTerminator| occurring in the pattern shall be escaped in _escapedPattern_ as necessary to ensure that the string-concatenation of *"/"*, _escapedPattern_, *"/"*, and _flags_ can be parsed (in an appropriate lexical context) as a |RegularExpressionLiteral| that behaves identically to the constructed regular expression. For example, if _pattern_ is *"/"*, then _escapedPattern_ could be *"\\/"* or *"\\u002F"*, among other possibilities, but not *"/"*, because `///` followed by _flags_ would be parsed as a |SingleLineComment| rather than a |RegularExpressionLiteral|. If _pattern_ is the empty String, this specification can be met by letting _escapedPattern_ be *"(?:)"*.
@@end

@@patch alg @sec-evaldeclarationinstantiation root/11:do:0..1

|           }
|           if declaredVariableNames fails $Contains(?name) {
|             perform $Append(?name, ?declaredVariableNames)
|           }
|         }
|       }
|     }
@@html

|     1. For each String _name_ of the BoundNames of _variableDecl_, do
|       1. If _declaredFuncNames_ does not contain _name_, then
|         1. If _variableEnv_ is a Global Environment Record, then
|           1. Let _variableDefinable_ be ? CanDeclareGlobalVar(_variableEnv_, _name_).
|           1. If _variableDefinable_ is *false*, throw a *TypeError* exception.

@@patch alg @sec-evaldeclarationinstantiation root/9:do:0..1
@@spectec
|     if variableDecl is not ty {|VariableDeclaration|; |ForBinding|; |BindingIdentifier|} {

|     1. NOTE: If there are multiple function declarations for the same name, the last declaration is used.
|     1. Let _funcName_ be the sole element of the BoundNames of _variableDecl_.
|     1. If _declaredFuncNames_ does not contain _funcName_, then
|       1. If _variableEnv_ is a Global Environment Record, then

|         1. If _funcDefinable_ is *false*, throw a *TypeError* exception.

@@patch alg @sec-evaldeclarationinstantiation root:12..13
@@spectec
|   if strict is val {false} and expr $"the host is a web browser"(data choice 0, clause @sec-block-level-function-declarations-web-legacy-compatibility-semantics) {
|     let declaredFuncOrVariableNames = $ListConcat([declaredFuncNames, declaredVariableNames])
|     for_each funcDecl in @ph-annexb-foreach(|FunctionDeclaration|, ?funcDecl, |StatementList|, [|Block|, |CaseClause|, |DefaultClause|], ?x, ?body, ?x) {
|       let funcName = sdo StringValue($ChildOf(|BindingIdentifier|, ?funcDecl))
|       if expr @ph-annexb-replacing(|FunctionDeclaration|, ?funcDecl, |VariableStatement|, ?funcName, |BindingIdentifier|, ?body) {
|         let bindingExists = false
|         set var thisEnv = lexicalEnv
|         assert pass "The following loop will terminate"
|         repeat_while thisEnv is not val {variableEnv} {
|           if thisEnv is not ty {#"Object Environment Record"} {
|             if ! thisEnv.HasBinding(funcName) is val {true} {
|               if expr $"the host is a web browser"(data choice 0, clause @sec-variablestatements-in-catch-blocks) {
|                 if thisEnv sat @ph-not-env-for(data choice 0, |Catch|) -> set var bindingExists = true
|               }
|               else {
|                 set var bindingExists = true
|               }
|             }
|           }
|           set var thisEnv = thisEnv[[OuterEnv]]
|         }
|         if bindingExists is val {false} and variableEnv is ty {#"Global Environment Record"} {
|           if @sec-haslexicaldeclaration(variableEnv, funcName) is val {false} {
|             let funcDefinable = ? @sec-candeclareglobalvar(variableEnv, funcName)
|           }
|           else {
|             let funcDefinable = false
|           }
|         }
|         else {
|           let funcDefinable = true
|         }
|         if bindingExists is val {false} and funcDefinable is val {true} {
|           if declaredFuncOrVariableNames fails $Contains(?funcName) {
|             if variableEnv is ty {#"Global Environment Record"} {
|               perform ? @sec-createglobalvarbinding(variableEnv, funcName, true)
|             }
|             else {
|               set var bindingExists = ! variableEnv.HasBinding(funcName)
|               if bindingExists is val {false} {
|                 perform ! variableEnv.CreateMutableBinding(funcName, true)
|                 perform ! variableEnv.InitializeBinding(funcName, undefined)
|               }
|             }
|             perform $Append(?funcName, ?declaredFuncOrVariableNames)
|           }
|           perform @ph-annexb-evaluated(|FunctionDeclaration|, ?funcDecl, |FunctionDeclaration|, clause @sec-function-definitions-runtime-semantics-evaluation, closure 0)
|         }
|       }
|     }
|   }
@@html
| 1. [id="step-evaldeclarationinstantiation-web-compat-insertion-point", normative-optional] If _strict_ is *false* and the host is a web browser or otherwise supports <emu-xref href="#sec-block-level-function-declarations-web-legacy-compatibility-semantics" title></emu-xref>, then
|   1. Let _declaredFuncOrVariableNames_ be the list-concatenation of _declaredFuncNames_ and _declaredVariableNames_.
|   1. For each |FunctionDeclaration| _funcDecl_ that is directly contained in the |StatementList| of any |Block|, |CaseClause|, or |DefaultClause| _x_ such that _body_ Contains _x_ is *true*, do
|     1. Let _funcName_ be the StringValue of the |BindingIdentifier| of _funcDecl_.
|     1. If replacing the |FunctionDeclaration| _funcDecl_ with a |VariableStatement| that has _funcName_ as a |BindingIdentifier| would not produce any Early Errors for _body_, then
|       1. Let _bindingExists_ be *false*.
|       1. Set _thisEnv_ to _lexicalEnv_.
|       1. Assert: The following loop will terminate.
|       1. Repeat, while _thisEnv_ is not _variableEnv_,
|         1. If _thisEnv_ is not an Object Environment Record, then
|           1. If ! _thisEnv_.HasBinding(_funcName_) is *true*, then
|             1. [id="step-evaldeclarationinstantiation-web-compat-bindingexists", normative-optional] If the host is a web browser or otherwise supports <emu-xref href="#sec-variablestatements-in-catch-blocks" title></emu-xref>, then
|               1. If _thisEnv_ is not the Environment Record for a |Catch| clause, set _bindingExists_ to *true*.
|             1. Else,
|               1. Set _bindingExists_ to *true*.
|         1. Set _thisEnv_ to _thisEnv_.[[OuterEnv]].
|       1. If _bindingExists_ is *false* and _variableEnv_ is a Global Environment Record, then
|         1. If HasLexicalDeclaration(_variableEnv_, _funcName_) is *false*, then
|           1. Let _funcDefinable_ be ? CanDeclareGlobalVar(_variableEnv_, _funcName_).
|         1. Else,
|           1. Let _funcDefinable_ be *false*.
|       1. Else,
|         1. Let _funcDefinable_ be *true*.
|       1. If _bindingExists_ is *false* and _funcDefinable_ is *true*, then
|         1. If _declaredFuncOrVariableNames_ does not contain _funcName_, then
|           1. If _variableEnv_ is a Global Environment Record, then
|             1. Perform ? CreateGlobalVarBinding(_variableEnv_, _funcName_, *true*).
|           1. Else,
|             1. Set _bindingExists_ to ! _variableEnv_.HasBinding(_funcName_).
|             1. If _bindingExists_ is *false*, then
|               1. Perform ! _variableEnv_.CreateMutableBinding(_funcName_, *true*).
|               1. Perform ! _variableEnv_.InitializeBinding(_funcName_, *undefined*).
|           1. Append _funcName_ to _declaredFuncOrVariableNames_.
|         1. [id="step-evaldeclarationinstantiation-alt-funcdecl-eval"] When the |FunctionDeclaration| _funcDecl_ is evaluated, perform the following steps in place of the |FunctionDeclaration| Evaluation algorithm provided in <emu-xref href="#sec-function-definitions-runtime-semantics-evaluation"></emu-xref>:
|           1. Let _globalEnv_ be the running execution context's VariableEnvironment.
|           1. Let _blockEnv_ be the running execution context's LexicalEnvironment.
|           1. Let _funcObj_ be ! _blockEnv_.GetBindingValue(_funcName_, *false*).
|           1. Perform ? <emu-meta effects="user-code">_globalEnv_.SetMutableBinding(_funcName_, _funcObj_, *false*)</emu-meta>.
|           1. Return ~unused~.
@@end

@@patch alg @sec-function.prototype.tostring root:1..4
@@spectec
|   if func is ty {Object} and func sat $HasSlot([[SourceText]]) and func[[SourceText]] is ty {#"sequence of Unicode code points"} and @sec-hosthassourcetextavailable(func) is val {true} {
|     return @sec-codepointstostring(func[[SourceText]])
|   }
|   if func is ty {sort [#"built-in"] #"function object"} {
|     if func sat $HasSlot([[InitialName]]) and func[[InitialName]] is ty {String} {
|       let name = func[[InitialName]]
|       let matched = false
|       if name is val {"get"} or name is val {"set"} {
|         set var matched = true
|       }
|       else_if name is not val {$"the empty String"()} {
|         let native = $ParseGoal(@sec-stringtocodepoints($"string-concatenation"("function ", name, "() { [native code] }")), |NativeFunction|, false, false, false)
|         let closed = $ParseGoal(@sec-stringtocodepoints($"string-concatenation"("function ", name, $CodeUnit(cu(10, "000A", "LINE FEED")), "() { [native code] }")), |NativeFunction|, false, false, false)
|         if native is not val {~empty~} and closed is not val {~empty~} and native sat @ph-contains-node(data choice 0, |PropertyName|) {
|           let key = @sec-codepointstostring($SourceText(@ph-first-node-within(|PropertyName|, ?native)))
|           if native sat @ph-not-contains-node(data choice 0, |NativeFunctionAccessor|) {
|             if name is val {key} -> set var matched = true
|           }
|           else {
|             let accessor = @sec-codepointstostring($SourceText(@ph-first-node-within(|NativeFunctionAccessor|, ?native)))
|             let n = $StrLen(?name)
|             if $Substring(?name, 0, $StrLen(?accessor)) is val {accessor} and $Substring(?name, n - $StrLen(?key), ?n) is val {key} -> set var matched = true
|           }
|         }
|       }
|       if name is not val {$"the empty String"()} and matched is val {false} -> return $""()
|     }
|     return $NativeFunctionSource(func)
|   }
|   if func is ty {Object} and @sec-iscallable(func) is val {true} -> return $NativeFunctionSource(func)
@@html
| 1. If _func_ is an Object, _func_ has a [[SourceText]] internal slot, _func_.[[SourceText]] is a sequence of Unicode code points, and HostHasSourceTextAvailable(_func_) is *true*, then
|   1. Return CodePointsToString(_func_.[[SourceText]]).
| 1. If _func_ is a <emu-xref href="#sec-built-in-function-objects">built-in function object</emu-xref>, return an implementation-defined String source code representation of _func_. The representation must have the syntax of a |NativeFunction|. Additionally, if _func_ has an [[InitialName]] internal slot and _func_.[[InitialName]] is a String, the portion of the returned String that would be matched by |NativeFunctionAccessor?| |PropertyName| must be _func_.[[InitialName]].
| 1. If _func_ is an Object and IsCallable(_func_) is *true*, return an implementation-defined String source code representation of _func_. The representation must have the syntax of a |NativeFunction|.
@@end

@@patch alg @sec-functiondeclarationinstantiation root/24:else:0..1
@@spectec
|     if expr $"the host is a web browser"(data choice 0, clause @sec-block-level-function-declarations-web-legacy-compatibility-semantics) {

|           1. Let _funcObj_ be ! _blockEnv_.GetBindingValue(_funcName_, *false*).

@@patch alg @sec-getsubstitution root/4:do:1..4
@@spectec
| if templateRemainder sat $StartsWith("$$") {
|       let #"ref" = "$$"
|       let refReplacement = "$"
|     }
|     else_if templateRemainder sat $StartsWith("$`") {
|       let #"ref" = "$`"
|       let refReplacement = $Substring(?string, 0, ?position)
|     }
|     else_if templateRemainder sat $StartsWith("$&") {
|       let #"ref" = "$&"
|       let refReplacement = matched
|     }
|     else_if expr @ph-starts-dollar-quote(?templateRemainder) {
|       let #"ref" = "$'"
|       let matchLength = $StrLen(?matched)
|       let tailPosition = position + matchLength
|       let refReplacement = $Substring(?string, $min(tailPosition, stringLength), data absent)
|       note "_tailPosition_ can exceed _stringLength_ only if this abstract operation was invoked by a call to the intrinsic %Symbol.replace% method of %RegExp.prototype% on an object whose *\"exec\"* property is not the intrinsic %RegExp.prototype.exec%."
|     }
|     else_if expr @ph-dollar-one-digit(?templateRemainder) {
|       if_else expr @ph-dollar-two-digits(?templateRemainder) { let digitCount = 2 } else { let digitCount = 1 }
|       let digits = $Substring(?templateRemainder, 1, 1 + digitCount)
|       let index = $ℝ(@sec-stringtonumber(digits))
|       assert expr $Chain(0, data choice 1, ?index, data choice 1, 99)
|       let captureLength = $ListLen(?#"captures")
|       if index > captureLength and digitCount == 2 {
|         note "When a two-digit replacement pattern specifies an index exceeding the count of capturing groups, it is treated as a one-digit replacement pattern followed by a literal digit."
|         set var digitCount = 1
|         set var digits = $Substring(?digits, 0, 1)
|         set var index = $ℝ(@sec-stringtonumber(digits))
|       }
|       let #"ref" = $Substring(?templateRemainder, 0, 1 + digitCount)
|       if expr $Chain(1, data choice 1, ?index, data choice 1, ?captureLength) {
|         let capture = #"captures"[index - 1]
|         if capture is val {undefined} {
|           let refReplacement = $"the empty String"()
|         }
|         else {
|           let refReplacement = capture
|         }
|       }
|       else {
|         let refReplacement = #"ref"
|       }
|     }
|     else_if templateRemainder sat $StartsWith("$<") {
|       let gtPosition = @sec-stringindexof(templateRemainder, ">", 0)
|       if gtPosition is val {~not-found~} or namedCaptures is val {undefined} {
|         let #"ref" = "$<"
|         let refReplacement = #"ref"
|       }
|       else {
|         let #"ref" = $Substring(?templateRemainder, 0, gtPosition + 1)
|         let groupName = $Substring(?templateRemainder, 2, ?gtPosition)
|         assert namedCaptures is ty {Object}
|         let capture = ? @sec-get-o-p(namedCaptures, groupName)
|         if capture is val {undefined} {
|           let refReplacement = $"the empty String"()
|         }
|         else {
|           let refReplacement = ? @sec-tostring(capture)
|         }
|       }
|     }
|     else {
|       let #"ref" = $Substring(?templateRemainder, 0, 1)
|       let refReplacement = #"ref"
|     }
| else_if #0 of root/4:do/1
| else_if #1 of root/4:do/1
@@html
|   1. If _templateRemainder_ starts with *"$$"*, then
|     1. Let _ref_ be *"$$"*.
|     1. Let _refReplacement_ be *"$"*.
|   1. Else if _templateRemainder_ starts with *"$`"*, then
|     1. Let _ref_ be *"$`"*.
|     1. Let _refReplacement_ be the substring of _string_ from 0 to _position_.
|   1. Else if _templateRemainder_ starts with *"$&amp;"*, then
|     1. Let _ref_ be *"$&amp;"*.
|     1. Let _refReplacement_ be _matched_.
@@end

@@patch alg @sec-getsubstitution root/4:do:6..7
@@spectec
| else_if #4 of root/4:do/1
@@html
|   1. Else if _templateRemainder_ starts with *"$&lt;"*, then
|     1. Let _gtPosition_ be StringIndexOf(_templateRemainder_, *">"*, 0).

@@patch alg @sec-globaldeclarationinstantiation root:11..12
@@spectec
|   if expr $"the host is a web browser"(data choice 0, clause @sec-block-level-function-declarations-web-legacy-compatibility-semantics) {
|     let strict = sdo ScriptIsStrict(script)
|     if strict is val {false} {
|       let declaredFuncOrVariableNames = $ListConcat([declaredFuncNames, declaredVariableNames])
|       for_each funcDecl in @ph-annexb-foreach(|FunctionDeclaration|, ?funcDecl, |StatementList|, [|Block|, |CaseClause|, |DefaultClause|], ?x, ?script, ?x) {
|         let funcName = sdo StringValue($ChildOf(|BindingIdentifier|, ?funcDecl))

|               perform @ph-annexb-evaluated(|FunctionDeclaration|, ?funcDecl, |FunctionDeclaration|, clause @sec-function-definitions-runtime-semantics-evaluation, closure 0)
|             }
|           }
|         }
|       }
|     }
|   }
@@html
| 1. [id="step-globaldeclarationinstantiation-web-compat-insertion-point", normative-optional] If the host is a web browser or otherwise supports <emu-xref href="#sec-block-level-function-declarations-web-legacy-compatibility-semantics" title></emu-xref>, then
|   1. Let _strict_ be ScriptIsStrict of _script_.
|   1. If _strict_ is *false*, then
|     1. Let _declaredFuncOrVariableNames_ be the list-concatenation of _declaredFuncNames_ and _declaredVariableNames_.
|     1. For each |FunctionDeclaration| _funcDecl_ that is directly contained in the |StatementList| of any |Block|, |CaseClause|, or |DefaultClause| _x_ such that _script_ Contains _x_ is *true*, do
|       1. Let _funcName_ be the StringValue of the |BindingIdentifier| of _funcDecl_.
|       1. If replacing the |FunctionDeclaration| _funcDecl_ with a |VariableStatement| that has _funcName_ as a |BindingIdentifier| would not produce any Early Errors for _script_, then

|               1. Append _funcName_ to _declaredFuncOrVariableNames_.
|             1. |               1. Let _globalEnv_ be the running execution context's VariableEnvironment.
|               1. Let _blockEnv_ be the running execution context's LexicalEnvironment.
|               1. Let _funcObj_ be ! _blockEnv_.GetBindingValue(_funcName_, *false*).
|               1. Perform ? <emu-meta effects="user-code">_globalEnv_.SetMutableBinding(_funcName_, _funcObj_, *false*)</emu-meta>.
|               1. Return ~unused~.
@@end

@@patch alg @sec-innermoduleevaluation root:10..11
@@spectec
|   for_each request : #"ModuleRequest Record" in module[[RequestedModules]] {
|     let requiredModule = @sec-GetImportedModule(module, request)
|     set var index = ? @sec-innermoduleevaluation(requiredModule, stack, index)
|     if requiredModule is ty {#"Cyclic Module Record"} {
|       assert requiredModule[[Status]] is val {~evaluating~; ~evaluating-async~; ~evaluated~}
|       assert expr @ph-iff(bool(requiredModule[[Status]] is val {~evaluating~}), bool(stack sat $Contains(?requiredModule)))
|       if requiredModule[[Status]] is val {~evaluating~} {
|         set field module[[DFSAncestorIndex]] = $min(module[[DFSAncestorIndex]], requiredModule[[DFSAncestorIndex]])
|       }
|       else {
|         set var requiredModule = requiredModule[[CycleRoot]]
|         assert requiredModule[[Status]] is val {~evaluating-async~; ~evaluated~}
|         if requiredModule[[EvaluationError]] is not val {~empty~} -> return ? requiredModule[[EvaluationError]]
|       }
|       if requiredModule[[AsyncEvaluationOrder]] is ty {integer} {
|         set field module[[PendingAsyncDependencies]] = module[[PendingAsyncDependencies]] + 1
|         perform $Append(?module, requiredModule[[AsyncParentModules]])
|       }
|     }
|   }
@@html
| 1. For each ModuleRequest Record _request_ of _module_.[[RequestedModules]], do
|   1. Let _requiredModule_ be GetImportedModule(_module_, _request_).
|   1. Set _index_ to ? InnerModuleEvaluation(_requiredModule_, _stack_, _index_).
|   1. If _requiredModule_ is a Cyclic Module Record, then
|     1. Assert: _requiredModule_.[[Status]] is one of ~evaluating~, ~evaluating-async~, or ~evaluated~.
|     1. Assert: _requiredModule_.[[Status]] is ~evaluating~ if and only if _stack_ contains _requiredModule_.
|     1. If _requiredModule_.[[Status]] is ~evaluating~, then
|       1. Set _module_.[[DFSAncestorIndex]] to min(_module_.[[DFSAncestorIndex]], _requiredModule_.[[DFSAncestorIndex]]).
|     1. Else,
|       1. Set _requiredModule_ to _requiredModule_.[[CycleRoot]].
|       1. Assert: _requiredModule_.[[Status]] is either ~evaluating-async~ or ~evaluated~.
|       1. If _requiredModule_.[[EvaluationError]] is not ~empty~, return ? _requiredModule_.[[EvaluationError]].
|     1. If _requiredModule_.[[AsyncEvaluationOrder]] is an integer, then
|       1. Set _module_.[[PendingAsyncDependencies]] to _module_.[[PendingAsyncDependencies]] + 1.
|       1. Append _module_ to _requiredModule_.[[AsyncParentModules]].
@@end

@@patch alg @sec-iterator.concat cl0:0..1
@@spectec
|   for_each iterable : Record in iterables {
|     let iterator = ? @sec-call(iterable[[OpenMethod]], iterable[[Iterable]])
|     if iterator is not ty {Object} -> throw @ph-new-error([[TypeError]])
|     let iteratorRecord = ? @sec-getiteratordirect(iterator)
|     let innerAlive = true
|     repeat_while innerAlive is val {true} {
|       let innerValue = ? @sec-iteratorstepvalue(iteratorRecord)
|       if innerValue is val {~done~} {
|         set var innerAlive = false
|       }
|       else {
|         let #"completion" = @sec-completion-ao(@sec-yield(innerValue))
|         if #"completion" is ty {#"abrupt completion"} {
|           return ? @sec-iteratorclose(iteratorRecord, #"completion")
|         }
|       }
|     }
|   }
@@html
|   1. For each Record _iterable_ of _iterables_, do
|     1. Let _iterator_ be ? Call(_iterable_.[[OpenMethod]], _iterable_.[[Iterable]]).
|     1. If _iterator_ is not an Object, throw a *TypeError* exception.
|     1. Let _iteratorRecord_ be ? GetIteratorDirect(_iterator_).
|     1. Let _innerAlive_ be *true*.
|     1. Repeat, while _innerAlive_ is *true*,
|       1. Let _innerValue_ be ? IteratorStepValue(_iteratorRecord_).
|       1. If _innerValue_ is ~done~, then
|         1. Set _innerAlive_ to *false*.
|       1. Else,
|         1. Let _completion_ be Completion(Yield(_innerValue_)).
|         1. If _completion_ is an abrupt completion, then
|           1. Return ? IteratorClose(_iteratorRecord_, _completion_).
@@end

@@patch alg @sec-makeday root:7..27
@@spectec
|   let inLeapYear = @sec-dayfromyear($ℝ(balancedYear) + 1) - @sec-dayfromyear($ℝ(balancedYear)) - 365
|   if balancedMonthMV == 0 -> let dayWithinYear = 0
|   else_if balancedMonthMV == 1 -> let dayWithinYear = 31
|   else_if balancedMonthMV == 2 -> let dayWithinYear = 59 + inLeapYear
|   else_if balancedMonthMV == 3 -> let dayWithinYear = 90 + inLeapYear
|   else_if balancedMonthMV == 4 -> let dayWithinYear = 120 + inLeapYear
|   else_if balancedMonthMV == 5 -> let dayWithinYear = 151 + inLeapYear
|   else_if balancedMonthMV == 6 -> let dayWithinYear = 181 + inLeapYear
|   else_if balancedMonthMV == 7 -> let dayWithinYear = 212 + inLeapYear
|   else_if balancedMonthMV == 8 -> let dayWithinYear = 243 + inLeapYear
|   else_if balancedMonthMV == 9 -> let dayWithinYear = 273 + inLeapYear
|   else_if balancedMonthMV == 10 -> let dayWithinYear = 304 + inLeapYear
|   else -> let dayWithinYear = 334 + inLeapYear
|   let firstDay = @sec-dayfromyear($ℝ(balancedYear)) + dayWithinYear
|   note "The Number nearest the middle of the day _firstDay_ lies in that day whenever some finite integral Number does."
|   let tv = $𝔽($TimeConst(data choice 6) * firstDay + $TimeConst(data choice 6) / 2)
|   if tv fails $IsFinite() -> return nan
|   if @sec-yearfromtime(tv) != $ℝ(balancedYear) -> return nan
|   if @sec-monthfromtime(tv) != balancedMonthMV -> return nan
|   if @sec-datefromtime(tv) != 1 -> return nan
@@html
| 1. Find a finite time value _tv_ such that YearFromTime(_tv_) = ℝ(_balancedYear_), MonthFromTime(_tv_) = _balancedMonthMV_, and DateFromTime(_tv_) = 1; but if this is not possible (because some argument is out of range), return *NaN*.
@@end

@@patch alg @sec-maybesimplecasefolding root:1..3
@@spectec
|   let otherSet = $NewRecord(data sort CharSet, data sort #"empty")
|   for_each s : CharSetElement in charSet {
|     let t = @ph-empty-character-sequence(data choice 1)
|     for_each codePoint : sort [single] #"code point" in s {
|       perform $Append($scf(codePoint), ?t)
|     }
|     perform $SetAdd(?t, ?otherSet)
|   }
@@html
| 1. Let _otherSet_ be a new empty CharSet.
| 1. For each CharSetElement _s_ of _charSet_, do
|   1. Let _t_ be an empty sequence of characters.
|   1. For each single code point _codePoint_ of _s_, do
|     1. Append scf(_codePoint_) to _t_.
|   1. Add _t_ to _otherSet_.
@@end

@@patch alg @sec-mightbothparticipate root:0..2
@@spectec
|   assert $NearestEnclosing(x, [|Pattern|]) is val {$NearestEnclosing(y, [|Pattern|])}
|   for_each d in $ParseNodesWithin($NearestEnclosing(x, [|Pattern|])) {
|     if expr $InstanceOf(d, prods [p"RegExp - Disjunction :: Alternative[?UnicodeMode, ?UnicodeSetsMode, ?NamedCaptureGroups] `|` Disjunction[?UnicodeMode, ?UnicodeSetsMode, ?NamedCaptureGroups]"]) {
|       let left = $ParseNodesWithin($ChildOf(|Alternative|, ?d))
|       let right = $ParseNodesWithin($ChildOf(|Disjunction|, ?d))
|       if left sat $Contains(?x) and right sat $Contains(?y) -> return false
|       if right sat $Contains(?x) and left sat $Contains(?y) -> return false
|     }
|   }
@@html
| 1. Assert: _x_ and _y_ have the same enclosing |Pattern|.
| 1. If the enclosing |Pattern| contains a <emu-grammar>Disjunction :: Alternative `|` Disjunction</emu-grammar> Parse Node such that either _x_ is contained within the |Alternative| and _y_ is contained within the derived |Disjunction|, or _x_ is contained within the derived |Disjunction| and _y_ is contained within the |Alternative|, return *false*.
@@end

@@patch alg @sec-number.prototype.toexponential root/9:else/0:else:0..3
@@spectec
|       let exponent = $NumberDigitsExponent($𝔽(number), 10) - 1
|       let ff = $NumberDigitsCount($𝔽(number), 10) - 1
|       let intSignificand = $NumberDigitsSignificand($𝔽(number), 10)
@@html
|     1. [id="step-number-proto-toexponential-intermediate-values"] Let _exponent_, _intSignificand_, and _ff_ be integers such that _ff_ ≥ 0, 10<sup>_ff_</sup> ≤ _intSignificand_ &lt; 10<sup>_ff_ + 1</sup>, 𝔽(_intSignificand_ × 10<sup>_exponent_ - _ff_</sup>) is 𝔽(_number_), and _ff_ is as small as possible. Note that the decimal representation of _intSignificand_ has _ff_ + 1 digits, _intSignificand_ is not divisible by 10, and the least significant digit of _intSignificand_ is not necessarily uniquely determined by these criteria.
@@end

@@patch alg @sec-number.prototype.toexponential root/9:else/0:then:0..5
@@spectec
|       let exponent = 0
|       repeat_while 10 ^ exponent > number {
|         set var exponent = exponent - 1
|       }
|       repeat_while 10 ^ {exponent + 1} <= number {
|         set var exponent = exponent + 1
|       }
|       let intSignificand = $floor(number / 10 ^ {exponent - fractionCount} + math(frac(0, 5, 1)))
|       if intSignificand == 10 ^ {fractionCount + 1} {
|         set var intSignificand = intSignificand / 10
|         set var exponent = exponent + 1
|       }
@@html
|     1. Let _exponent_ and _intSignificand_ be integers such that 10<sup>_fractionCount_</sup> ≤ _intSignificand_ &lt; 10<sup>_fractionCount_ + 1</sup> and for which _intSignificand_ × 10<sup>_exponent_ - _fractionCount_</sup> - _number_ is as close to zero as possible. If there are two such sets of _exponent_ and _intSignificand_, pick the _exponent_ and _intSignificand_ for which _intSignificand_ × 10<sup>_exponent_ - _fractionCount_</sup> is larger.
@@end

|   1. Set _significand_ to the string-concatenation of the first _exponent_ + 1 code units of _significand_, the code unit 0x002E (FULL STOP), and the remaining _precisionCount_ - (_exponent_ + 1) code units of _significand_.
@@end

@@patch alg @sec-number.prototype.toprecision root/9:else/6:then/1:then:0..2
@@spectec
|         let intPart = $FirstUnit(?significand)
|         let fractionalPart = @ph-other-code-units(data choice 0, precisionCount - 1, ?significand)
@@html
|       1. Let _intPart_ be the first code unit of _significand_.
|       1. Let _fractionalPart_ be the other _precisionCount_ - 1 code units of _significand_.

@@patch alg @sec-number.prototype.toprecision root/9:else:0..5
@@spectec
|     let exponent = 0
|     repeat_while 10 ^ exponent > number {
|       set var exponent = exponent - 1
|     }
|     repeat_while 10 ^ {exponent + 1} <= number {
|       set var exponent = exponent + 1
|     }
|     let intSignificand = $floor(number / 10 ^ {exponent - precisionCount + 1} + math(frac(0, 5, 1)))
|     if intSignificand == 10 ^ precisionCount {
|       set var intSignificand = intSignificand / 10
|       set var exponent = exponent + 1
|     }
@@html
|   1. Let _exponent_ and _intSignificand_ be integers such that 10<sup>_precisionCount_ - 1</sup> ≤ _intSignificand_ &lt; 10<sup>_precisionCount_</sup> and for which _intSignificand_ × 10<sup>_exponent_ - _precisionCount_ + 1</sup> - _number_ is as close to zero as possible. If there are two such sets of _exponent_ and _intSignificand_, pick the _exponent_ and _intSignificand_ for which _intSignificand_ × 10<sup>_exponent_ - _precisionCount_ + 1</sup> is larger.
@@end

@@patch alg @sec-number.prototype.toprecision root/9:then:0..1

@@html

@@patch alg @sec-numeric-types-number-tostring root:4..8
@@spectec
|   let n = $NumberDigitsExponent(x, radix)
|   let k = $NumberDigitsCount(x, radix)
|   let s = $NumberDigitsSignificand(x, radix)
|   if radix != 10 or n sat $InInterval(math(-5), 21) {
|     if n >= k {
|       return $"string-concatenation"(@ph-code-units-of(@ph-k-digits(?k, @ph-representation-radix(?s, ?radix))), @ph-occurrences-of(n - k, $CodeUnit(cu(48, "0030", "DIGIT ZERO"))))
|     }
|     if n > 0 {
|       return $"string-concatenation"(@ph-code-units-of(@ph-most-significant-k-digits(?n, @ph-representation-radix(?s, ?radix))), $CodeUnit(cu(46, "002E", "FULL STOP")), @ph-code-units-of(@ph-remaining-k-digits(k - n, @ph-representation-radix(?s, ?radix))))
|     }
|     assert n <= 0
|     return $"string-concatenation"($CodeUnit(cu(48, "0030", "DIGIT ZERO")), $CodeUnit(cu(46, "002E", "FULL STOP")), @ph-occurrences-of(neg n, $CodeUnit(cu(48, "0030", "DIGIT ZERO"))), @ph-code-units-of(@ph-k-digits(?k, @ph-representation-radix(?s, ?radix))))
|   }
@@html
| 1. [id="step-number-tostring-intermediate-values"] Let _n_, _k_, and _s_ be integers such that _k_ ≥ 1, _radix_<sup>_k_ - 1</sup> ≤ _s_ &lt; _radix_<sup>_k_</sup>, 𝔽(_s_ × _radix_<sup>_n_ - _k_</sup>) is _x_, and _k_ is as small as possible. Note that _k_ is the number of digits in the representation of _s_ using radix _radix_, that _s_ is not divisible by _radix_, and that the least significant digit of _s_ is not necessarily uniquely determined by these criteria.
| 1. If _radix_ ≠ 10 or _n_ is in the inclusive interval from -5 to 21, then
|   1. If _n_ ≥ _k_, then
|     1. Return the string-concatenation of:
|                   * the code units of the _k_ digits of the representation of _s_ using radix _radix_
|                   * _n_ - _k_ occurrences of the code unit 0x0030 (DIGIT ZERO)
|   1. If _n_ > 0, then
|     1. Return the string-concatenation of:
|                   * the code units of the most significant _n_ digits of the representation of _s_ using radix _radix_
|                   * the code unit 0x002E (FULL STOP)
|                   * the code units of the remaining _k_ - _n_ digits of the representation of _s_ using radix _radix_
|   1. Assert: _n_ ≤ 0.
|   1. Return the string-concatenation of:
|                 * the code unit 0x0030 (DIGIT ZERO)
|                 * the code unit 0x002E (FULL STOP)
|                 * -_n_ occurrences of the code unit 0x0030 (DIGIT ZERO)
|                 * the code units of the _k_ digits of the representation of _s_ using radix _radix_
@@end

@@patch alg @sec-numeric-types-number-tostring#1 root:0..1
@@spectec
|   perform $""()
@@html
| 1. Let _n_, _k_, and _s_ be integers such that _k_ ≥ 1, _radix_<sup>_k_ - 1</sup> ≤ _s_ &lt; _radix_<sup>_k_</sup>, 𝔽(_s_ × _radix_<sup>_n_ - _k_</sup>) is _x_, and _k_ is as small as possible. If there are multiple possibilities for _s_, choose _s_ such that _s_ × _radix_<sup>_n_ - _k_</sup> is closest to ℝ(_x_). If there are two such possible values of _s_, choose the one that is even. Note that _k_ is the number of digits in the representation of _s_ using radix _radix_ and that _s_ is not divisible by _radix_.
@@end

@@patch alg @sec-parsefloat-string root:3..10
@@spectec
|   let len = $StrLen(?trimmedString)
|   let i = 0
|   if i < len {
|     let sign = $NumericValue($UnitAt(?i, ?trimmedString))
|     if sign == math(hex(43, "002B")) or sign == math(hex(45, "002D")) -> set var i = i + 1
|   }
|   let end = 0
|   if i + 8 <= len and $Substring(?trimmedString, ?i, i + 8) is val {"Infinity"} {
|     set var end = i + 8
|   }
|   else {
|     let hasDigits = false
|     let done = false
|     repeat_while i < len and done is val {false} {
|       let u = $NumericValue($UnitAt(?i, ?trimmedString))
|       if u sat $InInterval(math(hex(48, "0030")), math(hex(57, "0039"))) {
|         set var hasDigits = true
|         set var i = i + 1
|       }
|       else {
|         set var done = true
|       }
|     }
|     if i < len {
|       let dot = $NumericValue($UnitAt(?i, ?trimmedString))
|       if dot == math(hex(46, "002E")) {
|         set var i = i + 1
|         let fracStart = i
|         set var done = false
|         repeat_while i < len and done is val {false} {
|           let u = $NumericValue($UnitAt(?i, ?trimmedString))
|           if_else u sat $InInterval(math(hex(48, "0030")), math(hex(57, "0039"))) { set var i = i + 1 } else { set var done = true }
|         }
|         if i > fracStart -> set var hasDigits = true
|       }
|     }
|     if hasDigits is val {false} -> return nan
|     set var end = i
|     if i < len {
|       let exp = $NumericValue($UnitAt(?i, ?trimmedString))
|       if exp == math(hex(101, "0065")) or exp == math(hex(69, "0045")) {
|         let j = i + 1
|         if j < len {
|           let es = $NumericValue($UnitAt(?j, ?trimmedString))
|           if es == math(hex(43, "002B")) or es == math(hex(45, "002D")) -> set var j = j + 1
|         }
|         let expStart = j
|         set var done = false
|         repeat_while j < len and done is val {false} {
|           let u = $NumericValue($UnitAt(?j, ?trimmedString))
|           if_else u sat $InInterval(math(hex(48, "0030")), math(hex(57, "0039"))) { set var j = j + 1 } else { set var done = true }
|         }
|         if j > expStart -> set var end = j
|       }
|     }
|   }
|   let trimmedPrefix = @sec-stringtocodepoints($Substring(?trimmedString, 0, ?end))
@@html
| 1. Let _trimmedPrefix_ be the longest prefix of _trimmed_ that satisfies the syntax of a |StrDecimalLiteral|, which might be _trimmed_ itself. If there is no such prefix, return *NaN*.
@@end

|   }
@@html

@@patch alg @sec-parseint-string-radix root:14..18
@@spectec
|   let mathInt = 0
|   for_range i incl 0 .. excl $StrLen(?numberString) {
|     let d = @formal-radixdigitvalue($UnitAt(?i, ?numberString))
|     set var mathInt = mathInt * radixMV + d
|   }
|   if radixMV == 10 and mathInt >= 10 ^ 20 {
|     let q = 10 ^ {$DecimalExponent(mathInt) - 19}
|     set var mathInt = $"implementation-defined choice"(?mathInt, $floor(mathInt / q) * q)
|   }
|   if radixMV is not val {2; 4; 8; 10; 16; 32} -> set var mathInt = $ApproxInteger(mathInt)
@@html
| 1. Let _mathInt_ be the integer value that is represented by _numberString_ in radix-_radixMV_ notation, using the letters <b>A</b> through <b>Z</b> and <b>a</b> through <b>z</b> for digits with values 10 through 35. (However, if _radixMV_ = 10 and _numberString_ contains more than 20 significant digits, every significant digit after the 20th may be replaced by a 0 digit, at the option of the implementation; and if _radixMV_ is not one of 2, 4, 8, 10, 16, or 32, then _mathInt_ may be an implementation-approximated integer representing the integer value denoted by _numberString_ in radix-_radixMV_ notation.)
@@end

@@patch alg @sec-parseint-string-radix root:6..8

|   if $FirstUnit(?trimmedString) is val {$CodeUnit(cu(45, "002D", "HYPHEN-MINUS"))} {

|     set var trimmedString = $Substring(?trimmedString, 1, data absent)
|   }
|   else_if $FirstUnit(?trimmedString) is val {$CodeUnit(cu(43, "002B", "PLUS SIGN"))} {
|     set var trimmedString = $Substring(?trimmedString, 1, data absent)
|   }
@@html
| 1. If the first code unit of _trimmedString_ is the code unit 0x002D (HYPHEN-MINUS), then
|   1. Set _sign_ to -1.

|   1. Set _trimmedString_ to the substring of _trimmedString_ from 1.
@@end

@@patch alg @sec-parsemodule root:9..10
@@spectec
|   for_each exportEntry : #"ExportEntry Record" in exportEntries {
|     if exportEntry[[ModuleRequest]] is val {null} {
|       if importedBoundNames fails $Contains(exportEntry[[LocalName]]) {
|         perform $Append(?exportEntry, ?localExportEntries)
|       }
|       else {
|         note "When exporting a binding or namespace object which was originally imported from another module, the ExportEntry Record is rewritten to match the form it would have if the binding or namespace object had been re-exported directly from the original module rather than imported then exported. This allows conflicts which arise from exporting the same binding or namespace twice under the same name through `export * from` to be ignored rather than being treated as ambiguous in step <emu-xref href=\"#step-resolveexport-conflict\"></emu-xref> of <emu-xref href=\"#sec-resolveexport\">the ResolveExport concrete method of Source Text Module Records</emu-xref>."
|         let importEntry = @ph-elem-whose(?importEntries, [[LocalName]], data choice 1, exportEntry[[LocalName]])
|         perform $Append(record #"ExportEntry Record" { [[ModuleRequest]]: importEntry[[ModuleRequest]], [[ImportName]]: importEntry[[ImportName]], [[LocalName]]: null, [[ExportName]]: exportEntry[[ExportName]] }, ?indirectExportEntries)
|       }
|     }
|     else_if exportEntry[[ImportName]] is val {~all-but-default~} {
|       assert exportEntry[[ExportName]] is val {null}
|       perform $Append(?exportEntry, ?starExportEntries)
|     }
|     else {
|       perform $Append(?exportEntry, ?indirectExportEntries)
|     }
|   }
@@html
| 1. For each ExportEntry Record _exportEntry_ of _exportEntries_, do
|   1. If _exportEntry_.[[ModuleRequest]] is *null*, then
|     1. If _importedBoundNames_ does not contain _exportEntry_.[[LocalName]], then
|       1. Append _exportEntry_ to _localExportEntries_.
|     1. Else,
|       1. NOTE: When exporting a binding or namespace object which was originally imported from another module, the ExportEntry Record is rewritten to match the form it would have if the binding or namespace object had been re-exported directly from the original module rather than imported then exported. This allows conflicts which arise from exporting the same binding or namespace twice under the same name through `export * from` to be ignored rather than being treated as ambiguous in step <emu-xref href="#step-resolveexport-conflict"></emu-xref> of <emu-xref href="#sec-resolveexport">the ResolveExport concrete method of Source Text Module Records</emu-xref>.
|       1. Let _importEntry_ be the element of _importEntries_ whose [[LocalName]] is _exportEntry_.[[LocalName]].
|       1. Append the ExportEntry Record { [[ModuleRequest]]: _importEntry_.[[ModuleRequest]], [[ImportName]]: _importEntry_.[[ImportName]], [[LocalName]]: *null*, [[ExportName]]: _exportEntry_.[[ExportName]] } to _indirectExportEntries_.
|   1. Else if _exportEntry_.[[ImportName]] is ~all-but-default~, then
|     1. Assert: _exportEntry_.[[ExportName]] is *null*.
|     1. Append _exportEntry_ to _starExportEntries_.
|   1. Else,
|     1. Append _exportEntry_ to _indirectExportEntries_.
@@end

@@patch alg @sec-parsepattern root:0..1
@@spectec
|   if expr $"the host is a web browser"(data choice 0, clause @sec-regular-expressions-patterns) -> return @sec-parsepattern-annexb(patternText, u, v)
@@html

@@patch alg @sec-parsepattern-annexb root/0:else:0..2
@@spectec
|     let parseResult = @sec-parsetext(patternText, |Pattern[~UnicodeMode, ~UnicodeSetsMode, ~NamedCaptureGroups]|)
|     if parseResult is ty {#"Parse Node"} and expr @ph-contains-cond(?parseResult, |GroupName|) {

@@html

|   1. <ins>If _parseResult_ is a Parse Node and _parseResult_ contains a |GroupName|, then</ins>
|     1. Set _parseResult_ to ParseText(_patternText_, |Pattern[~UnicodeMode, ~UnicodeSetsMode, +NamedCaptureGroups]|).
@@end

@@patch alg @sec-parsetext root:1..3
@@spectec
|   return @formal-parsetextin(sourceText, goalSymbol, false, false, false)
|   return $""()
@@html
| 1. Attempt to <emu-xref href="#sec-parsing">parse</emu-xref> _sourceText_ using _goalSymbol_ as the goal symbol, and analyse the parse result for any early error conditions. Parsing and early error detection may be interleaved in an implementation-defined manner.
| 1. NOTE: When _goalSymbol_ is a nonterminal of the syntactic grammar, parsing includes conversion of _sourceText_ from code points to a stream of input elements as described in <emu-xref href="#sec-syntactic-grammar"></emu-xref>.
| 1. If the parse succeeded and no early errors were found, return the Parse Node (an instance of _goalSymbol_) at the root of the parse tree resulting from the parse.
| 1. Return a List of one or more *SyntaxError* objects representing the parsing errors and/or early errors. If more than one parsing error or early error is present, the number and ordering of error objects in the list is implementation-defined, but at least one must be present.
@@end

@@patch alg @sec-performeval root:11..20
@@spectec
|   if_else direct is val {~direct-eval~} { let directEval = true } else { let directEval = false }
|   let script = @formal-parsetextin(sourceString, |Script|, strictCaller, false, directEval)
|   if script is ty {list<error>} -> throw @ph-new-error([[SyntaxError]])
|   if @ph-contains(?script, |ScriptBody|) is val {false} -> return undefined
|   let body = $ChildOf(|ScriptBody|, ?script)
|   if inFunc is val {false} and expr @ph-contains-cond(?body, |NewTarget|) -> throw @ph-new-error([[SyntaxError]])
|   if inMethod is val {false} and expr @ph-contains-cond(?body, |SuperProperty|) -> throw @ph-new-error([[SyntaxError]])
|   if inDerivedCtor is val {false} and expr @ph-contains-cond(?body, |SuperCall|) -> throw @ph-new-error([[SyntaxError]])
|   if inClassFieldInitializer is val {true} and sdo ContainsArguments(body) is val {true} -> throw @ph-new-error([[SyntaxError]])
@@html
| 1. Perform the following substeps in an implementation-defined order, possibly interleaving parsing and error detection:
|   1. Let _script_ be ParseText(_sourceString_, |Script|).
|   1. If _script_ is a List of errors, throw a *SyntaxError* exception.
|   1. If _script_ Contains |ScriptBody| is *false*, return *undefined*.
|   1. Let _body_ be the |ScriptBody| of _script_.
|   1. If _inFunc_ is *false* and _body_ Contains |NewTarget|, throw a *SyntaxError* exception.
|   1. If _inMethod_ is *false* and _body_ Contains |SuperProperty|, throw a *SyntaxError* exception.
|   1. If _inDerivedCtor_ is *false* and _body_ Contains |SuperCall|, throw a *SyntaxError* exception.
|   1. If _inClassFieldInitializer_ is *true* and ContainsArguments of _body_ is *true*, throw a *SyntaxError* exception.
@@end

@@patch alg @sec-performeval root:21..22
@@spectec
| else of root/20
@@html
| 1. Else, let _strictEval_ be ScriptIsStrict of _script_.
@@end

|   set field evalContext[[VariableEnvironment]] = variableEnv
|   set field evalContext[[LexicalEnvironment]] = lexicalEnv
|   set field evalContext[[PrivateEnvironment]] = privateEnv
@@html

| 1. Let _evalContext_ be a new ECMAScript code execution context.
| 1. Set _evalContext_'s Function to *null*.
| 1. Set _evalContext_'s Realm to _evalRealm_.
| 1. Set _evalContext_'s ScriptOrModule to _runningContext_'s ScriptOrModule.

@@end

@@patch alg @sec-quotejsonstring root:1..2
@@spectec
|   for_each codePoint : #"code point" in @sec-stringtocodepoints(value) {
|     if codePoint sat @ph-listed-in-column(code "Code Point", table t"table-json-single-character-escapes") {
|       set var product = $"string-concatenation"(product, $TableLookup(table t"table-json-single-character-escapes", code "Escape Sequence", code "Code Point", ?codePoint))
|     }
|     else_if codePoint sat @ph-value-less-than(cu(32, "0020", "SPACE")) or codePoint sat @ph-surrogate-value() {
|       let unit = $UnitOrPointOf(data choice 1, $NumericValue(?codePoint))
|       set var product = $"string-concatenation"(product, @sec-unicodeescape(unit))
|     }
|     else {
|       set var product = $"string-concatenation"(product, @sec-utf16encodecodepoint(codePoint))
|     }
|   }
@@html
| 1. For each code point _codePoint_ of StringToCodePoints(_value_), do
|   1. If _codePoint_ is listed in the “Code Point” column of <emu-xref href="#table-json-single-character-escapes"></emu-xref>, then
|     1. Set _product_ to the string-concatenation of _product_ and the escape sequence for _codePoint_ as specified in the “Escape Sequence” column of the corresponding row.
|   1. Else if _codePoint_ has a numeric value less than 0x0020 (SPACE) or _codePoint_ has the same numeric value as a leading surrogate or trailing surrogate, then
|     1. Let _unit_ be the code unit whose numeric value is the numeric value of _codePoint_.
|     1. Set _product_ to the string-concatenation of _product_ and UnicodeEscape(_unit_).
|   1. Else,
|     1. Set _product_ to the string-concatenation of _product_ and UTF16EncodeCodePoint(_codePoint_).
@@end

@@patch alg @sec-races root/0:then:0..1
@@spectec
|     if expr @ph-not-the-case(data choice 0, bool(expr $""(?eventA, data choice 0, ?eventB, ?execution) and expr $""(?eventB, data choice 0, ?eventA, ?execution))) {
|       if eventA is ty {#"WriteSharedMemory event"} or eventA is ty {ReadModifyWriteSharedMemory} {
|         if eventB is ty {#"WriteSharedMemory event"} or eventB is ty {ReadModifyWriteSharedMemory} {
|           if expr @ph-not-disjoint-memory-ranges(?eventA, ?eventB) -> return true
|         }
|       }
|       if expr $""(?eventA, data choice 1, ?eventB, ?execution) or expr $""(?eventB, data choice 1, ?eventA, ?execution) {
|         return true
|       }
|     }
@@html
|   1. If it is not the case that both _eventA_ happens-before _eventB_ in _execution_ and _eventB_ happens-before _eventA_ in _execution_, then
|     1. If _eventA_ is either a WriteSharedMemory or ReadModifyWriteSharedMemory event, _eventB_ is either a WriteSharedMemory or ReadModifyWriteSharedMemory event, and _eventA_ and _eventB_ do not have disjoint memory ranges, then
|       1. Return *true*.
|     1. If _eventA_ reads-from _eventB_ in _execution_ or _eventB_ reads-from _eventA_ in _execution_, then
|       1. Return *true*.
@@end

@@patch alg @sec-regexp.escape root:3..4
@@spectec
|   for_each codePoint : #"code point" in codePointList {
|     if escaped is val {$"the empty String"()} and codePoint sat @ph-matched-by-either([|DecimalDigit|, |AsciiLetter|]) {
|       note "Escaping a leading digit ensures that output corresponds with pattern text which may be used after a `\\0` character escape or a |DecimalEscape| such as `\\1` and still match _string_ rather than be interpreted as an extension of the preceding escape sequence. Escaping a leading ASCII letter does the same for the context after `\\c`."
|       let numericValue = $NumericValue(?codePoint)
|       let #"hex" = @sec-numeric-types-number-tostring($𝔽(numericValue), 16)
|       assert $StrLen(?#"hex") is val {2}
|       set var escaped = $"string-concatenation"($CodeUnit(cu(92, "005C", "REVERSE SOLIDUS")), "x", #"hex")
|     }
|     else {
|       set var escaped = $"string-concatenation"(escaped, @sec-encodeforregexpescape(codePoint))
|     }
|   }
@@html
| 1. For each code point _codePoint_ of _codePointList_, do
|   1. If _escaped_ is the empty String and _codePoint_ is matched by either |DecimalDigit| or |AsciiLetter|, then
|     1. NOTE: Escaping a leading digit ensures that output corresponds with pattern text which may be used after a `\0` character escape or a |DecimalEscape| such as `\1` and still match _string_ rather than be interpreted as an extension of the preceding escape sequence. Escaping a leading ASCII letter does the same for the context after `\c`.
|     1. Let _numericValue_ be the numeric value of _codePoint_.
|     1. Let _hex_ be Number::toString(𝔽(_numericValue_), 16).
|     1. Assert: The length of _hex_ is 2.
|     1. Set _escaped_ to the string-concatenation of the code unit 0x005C (REVERSE SOLIDUS), *"x"*, and _hex_.
|   1. Else,
|     1. Set _escaped_ to the string-concatenation of _escaped_ and EncodeForRegExpEscape(_codePoint_).
@@end

@@patch alg @sec-resolveexport root:4..6
@@spectec
|   for_each exportEntry : #"ExportEntry Record" in module[[LocalExportEntries]] {
|     if exportEntry[[ExportName]] is val {exportName} {
|       assert module[[LocalExportEntries]] sat $Contains(?exportEntry)
|       return record #"ResolvedBinding Record" { [[Module]]: module, [[BindingName]]: exportEntry[[LocalName]] }
|     }
|   }
|   for_each exportEntry : #"ExportEntry Record" in module[[IndirectExportEntries]] {
|     if exportEntry[[ExportName]] is val {exportName} {
|       assert exportEntry[[ModuleRequest]] is not val {null}
|       let importedModule = @sec-GetImportedModule(module, exportEntry[[ModuleRequest]])
|       if exportEntry[[ImportName]] is val {~namespace~} {
|         assert module[[LocalExportEntries]] fails $Contains(?exportEntry)
|         return record #"ResolvedBinding Record" { [[Module]]: importedModule, [[BindingName]]: ~namespace~ }
|       }
|       assert module[[IndirectExportEntries]] sat $Contains(?exportEntry)
|       assert exportEntry[[ImportName]] is ty {String}
|       return importedModule.ResolveExport(exportEntry[[ImportName]], resolveSet)
|     }
|   }
@@html
| 1. For each ExportEntry Record _exportEntry_ of _module_.[[LocalExportEntries]], do
|   1. If _exportEntry_.[[ExportName]] is _exportName_, then
|     1. Assert: _module_ provides the direct binding for this export.
|     1. Return ResolvedBinding Record { [[Module]]: _module_, [[BindingName]]: _exportEntry_.[[LocalName]] }.
| 1. For each ExportEntry Record _exportEntry_ of _module_.[[IndirectExportEntries]], do
|   1. If _exportEntry_.[[ExportName]] is _exportName_, then
|     1. Assert: _exportEntry_.[[ModuleRequest]] is not *null*.
|     1. Let _importedModule_ be GetImportedModule(_module_, _exportEntry_.[[ModuleRequest]]).
|     1. If _exportEntry_.[[ImportName]] is ~namespace~, then
|       1. Assert: _module_ does not provide the direct binding for this export.
|       1. Return ResolvedBinding Record { [[Module]]: _importedModule_, [[BindingName]]: ~namespace~ }.
|     1. Assert: _module_ imports a specific binding for this export.
|     1. Assert: _exportEntry_.[[ImportName]] is a String.
|     1. Return _importedModule_.ResolveExport(_exportEntry_.[[ImportName]], _resolveSet_).
@@end

@@patch alg @sec-resolveexport root:8..9
@@spectec
|   for_each exportEntry : #"ExportEntry Record" in module[[StarExportEntries]] {
|     assert exportEntry[[ModuleRequest]] is not val {null}
|     let importedModule = @sec-GetImportedModule(module, exportEntry[[ModuleRequest]])
|     let resolution = importedModule.ResolveExport(exportName, resolveSet)
|     if resolution is val {~ambiguous~} -> return ~ambiguous~
|     if resolution is not val {null} {
|       assert resolution is ty {#"ResolvedBinding Record"}
|       if starResolution is val {null} {
|         set var starResolution = resolution
|       }
|       else {
|         assert pass "There is more than one `*` export that includes the requested name"
|         if expr @ph-not-same-tn(resolution[[Module]], starResolution[[Module]], data sort #"Module Record") -> return ~ambiguous~
|         if resolution[[BindingName]] is not val {starResolution[[BindingName]]} -> return ~ambiguous~
|       }
|     }
|   }
@@html
| 1. For each ExportEntry Record _exportEntry_ of _module_.[[StarExportEntries]], do
|   1. Assert: _exportEntry_.[[ModuleRequest]] is not *null*.
|   1. Let _importedModule_ be GetImportedModule(_module_, _exportEntry_.[[ModuleRequest]]).
|   1. Let _resolution_ be _importedModule_.ResolveExport(_exportName_, _resolveSet_).
|   1. If _resolution_ is ~ambiguous~, return ~ambiguous~.
|   1. If _resolution_ is not *null*, then
|     1. Assert: _resolution_ is a ResolvedBinding Record.
|     1. If _starResolution_ is *null*, then
|       1. Set _starResolution_ to _resolution_.
|     1. [id="step-resolveexport-conflict"] Else,
|       1. Assert: There is more than one `*` export that includes the requested name.
|       1. If _resolution_.[[Module]] and _starResolution_.[[Module]] are not the same Module Record, return ~ambiguous~.
|       1. If _resolution_.[[BindingName]] is not _starResolution_.[[BindingName]], return ~ambiguous~.
@@end

@@patch alg @sec-runtime-semantics-forin-div-ofbodyevaluation-lhs-stmt-iterator-lhskind-labelset root/6:do:6..7
@@spectec
| if lhsKind is val {~assignment~; ~var-binding~} {
|       if destructuring is val {true} {
|         if lhsKind is val {~assignment~} {
|           let status = @sec-completion-ao(sdo DestructuringAssignmentEvaluation(assignmentPattern; nextValue))
|         }
|         else {
|           assert lhsKind is val {~var-binding~}
|           assert lhs is ty {|ForBinding|}
|           let status = @sec-completion-ao(sdo BindingInitialization(lhs; nextValue, undefined))
|         }
|       }
|       else {
|         let lhsRef = @sec-completion-ao(sdo Evaluation(lhs))
|         if lhsKind is val {~assignment~} and sdo AssignmentTargetType(lhs) is val {~web-compat~} -> throw @ph-new-error([[ReferenceError]])
|         if lhsRef is ty {#"abrupt completion"} {
|           let status = lhsRef
|         }
|         else {
|           let status = @sec-completion-ao(@sec-putvalue(lhsRef[[Value]], nextValue))
|         }
|       }
|       let iterationEnv = undefined
|     }
|     else {
|       assert lhsKind is val {~lexical-binding~}
|       assert lhs is ty {|ForDeclaration|}
|       let iterationEnv = @sec-newdeclarativeenvironment(oldEnv)
|       perform sdo ForDeclarationBindingInstantiation(lhs; iterationEnv)
|       set field $Running()[[LexicalEnvironment]] = iterationEnv
|       if destructuring is val {true} {
|         let status = @sec-completion-ao(sdo ForDeclarationBindingInitialization(lhs; nextValue, iterationEnv))
|       }
|       else {
|         assert $ListLen(sdo BoundNames(lhs)) is val {1}
|         let lhsName = $ListElem(data choice 0, sdo BoundNames(lhs))
|         let lhsRef = ! @sec-resolvebinding(lhsName)
|         if declKind is not val {~normal~} {
|           assert @sec-isunresolvablereference(lhsRef) is val {false}
|           let base = lhsRef[[Base]]
|           assert base is ty {#"Declarative Environment Record"}
|           let status = @sec-completion-ao(@sec-adddisposableresource(base[[DisposableResourceStack]], nextValue, declKind))
|         }
|         else {
|           let status = @sec-normalcompletion(~unused~)
|         }
|         if status is ty {#"normal completion"} {
|           set var status = @sec-completion-ao(@sec-initializereferencedbinding(lhsRef, nextValue))
|         }
|       }
|     }
@@html
|   1. If _lhsKind_ is either ~assignment~ or ~var-binding~, then
|     1. If _destructuring_ is *true*, then
|       1. If _lhsKind_ is ~assignment~, then
|         1. Let _status_ be Completion(DestructuringAssignmentEvaluation of _assignmentPattern_ with argument _nextValue_).
|       1. Else,
|         1. Assert: _lhsKind_ is ~var-binding~.
|         1. Assert: _lhs_ is a |ForBinding|.
|         1. Let _status_ be Completion(BindingInitialization of _lhs_ with arguments _nextValue_ and *undefined*).
|     1. Else,
|       1. Let _lhsRef_ be Completion(Evaluation of _lhs_). (It may be evaluated repeatedly.)
|       1. If _lhsKind_ is ~assignment~ and the AssignmentTargetType of _lhs_ is ~web-compat~, throw a *ReferenceError* exception.
|       1. If _lhsRef_ is an abrupt completion, then
|         1. Let _status_ be _lhsRef_.
|       1. Else,
|         1. Let _status_ be Completion(PutValue(_lhsRef_.[[Value]], _nextValue_)).
|     1. Let _iterationEnv_ be *undefined*.
@@end

@@patch alg @sec-shallowestcontainedjsonvalue root/7:do:3..4
@@spectec
|     for_each type : nonterminal in types {
|       if expr $InstanceOf(candidate, ?type) {
|         note "In the JSON grammar, a <code>number</code> token may represent a negative value. In ECMAScript, negation is represented as a unary operation in which a |UnaryExpression| parses to a `-` followed by a derived |UnaryExpression|."
|         if type is val {|UnaryExpression|} {
|           if $Parent(candidate) is not ty {pred(sat @ph-type-nt-parse-node(data choice 0, |UnaryExpression|))} -> set var unaryExpr = candidate
|         }
|         else_if type is val {|NumericLiteral|} {
|           assert $ParseNodesWithin(unaryExpr) sat $Contains(?candidate)
|           return unaryExpr
|         }
|         else {
|           return candidate
|         }
|       }
|       if queuedChildren is val {false} and expr $InstanceOf(candidate, data sort nonterminal) and @ph-contains(?candidate, ?type) is val {true} {
|         let #"children" = $ChildNodes(candidate)
|         set var queue = $ListConcat([queue, #"children"])
|         set var queuedChildren = true
|       }
|     }
@@html
|   1. For each nonterminal _type_ of _types_, do
|     1. If _candidate_ is an instance of _type_, then
|       1. NOTE: In the JSON grammar, a <code>number</code> token may represent a negative value. In ECMAScript, negation is represented as a unary operation in which a |UnaryExpression| parses to a `-` followed by a derived |UnaryExpression|.
|       1. If _type_ is |UnaryExpression|, then
|         1. If the parent of _candidate_ is not a |UnaryExpression| Parse Node, set _unaryExpr_ to _candidate_.
|       1. Else if _type_ is |NumericLiteral|, then
|         1. Assert: _candidate_ is contained within _unaryExpr_.
|         1. Return _unaryExpr_.
|       1. Else,
|         1. Return _candidate_.
|     1. If _queuedChildren_ is *false*, _candidate_ is an instance of a nonterminal, and _candidate_ Contains _type_ is *true*, then
|       1. Let _children_ be a List containing each child node of _candidate_, in order.
|       1. Set _queue_ to the list-concatenation of _queue_ and _children_.
|       1. Set _queuedChildren_ to *true*.
@@end
