@@patch alg @sec-source-text-module-record-initialize-environment root:6..8
@@spectec
|   for_each importEntry : #"ImportEntry Record" in module[[ImportEntries]] {
|     let importedModule = @sec-GetImportedModule(module, importEntry[[ModuleRequest]])
|     if importEntry[[ImportName]] is val {~namespace~} {
|       let namespace = @sec-getmodulenamespace(importedModule)
|       perform ! envRecord.CreateImmutableBinding(importEntry[[LocalName]], true)
|       perform ! envRecord.InitializeBinding(importEntry[[LocalName]], namespace)
|     }
|     else {
|       assert importEntry[[ImportName]] is ty {String}
|       let resolution = importedModule.ResolveExport(importEntry[[ImportName]])
|       if resolution is val {null; ~ambiguous~} -> throw @ph-new-error([[SyntaxError]])
|       if resolution[[BindingName]] is val {~namespace~} {
|         let namespace = @sec-getmodulenamespace(resolution[[Module]])
|         perform ! envRecord.CreateImmutableBinding(importEntry[[LocalName]], true)
|         perform ! envRecord.InitializeBinding(importEntry[[LocalName]], namespace)
|       }
|       else {
|         perform @sec-createimportbinding(envRecord, importEntry[[LocalName]], resolution[[Module]], resolution[[BindingName]])
|       }
|     }
|   }
|   let moduleContext = $NewRecord(data sort #"ECMAScript code execution context")
@@html
| 1. For each ImportEntry Record _importEntry_ of _module_.[[ImportEntries]], do
|   1. Let _importedModule_ be GetImportedModule(_module_, _importEntry_.[[ModuleRequest]]).
|   1. If _importEntry_.[[ImportName]] is ~namespace~, then
|     1. Let _namespace_ be GetModuleNamespace(_importedModule_).
|     1. Perform ! _envRecord_.CreateImmutableBinding(_importEntry_.[[LocalName]], *true*).
|     1. Perform ! _envRecord_.InitializeBinding(_importEntry_.[[LocalName]], _namespace_).
|   1. Else,
|     1. Assert: _importEntry_.[[ImportName]] is a String.
|     1. Let _resolution_ be _importedModule_.ResolveExport(_importEntry_.[[ImportName]]).
|     1. If _resolution_ is either *null* or ~ambiguous~, throw a *SyntaxError* exception.
|     1. If _resolution_.[[BindingName]] is ~namespace~, then
|       1. Let _namespace_ be GetModuleNamespace(_resolution_.[[Module]]).
|       1. Perform ! _envRecord_.CreateImmutableBinding(_importEntry_.[[LocalName]], *true*).
|       1. Perform ! _envRecord_.InitializeBinding(_importEntry_.[[LocalName]], _namespace_).
|     1. Else,
|       1. Perform CreateImportBinding(_envRecord_, _importEntry_.[[LocalName]], _resolution_.[[Module]], _resolution_.[[BindingName]]).
| 1. Let _moduleContext_ be a new ECMAScript code execution context.
@@end

@@patch alg @sec-toobject root:0..6
@@spectec
|   if arg is val {undefined; null} -> throw @ph-new-error([[TypeError]])
|   if arg is ty {Boolean} {
|     let #"obj" = @sec-ordinaryobjectcreate(intrinsic [[Boolean.prototype]], [[[BooleanData]]])
|     set field #"obj"[[BooleanData]] = arg
|     return #"obj"
|   }
|   if arg is ty {Number} {
|     let #"obj" = @sec-ordinaryobjectcreate(intrinsic [[Number.prototype]], [[[NumberData]]])
|     set field #"obj"[[NumberData]] = arg
|     return #"obj"
|   }
|   if arg is ty {String} -> return @sec-stringcreate(arg, intrinsic [[String.prototype]])
|   if arg is ty {Symbol} {
|     let #"obj" = @sec-ordinaryobjectcreate(intrinsic [[Symbol.prototype]], [[[SymbolData]]])
|     set field #"obj"[[SymbolData]] = arg
|     return #"obj"
|   }
|   if arg is ty {BigInt} {
|     let #"obj" = @sec-ordinaryobjectcreate(intrinsic [[BigInt.prototype]], [[[BigIntData]]])
|     set field #"obj"[[BigIntData]] = arg
|     return #"obj"
|   }
@@html
| 1. If _arg_ is either *undefined* or *null*, throw a *TypeError* exception.
| 1. If _arg_ is a Boolean, return a new Boolean object whose [[BooleanData]] internal slot is set to _arg_. See <emu-xref href="#sec-boolean-objects"></emu-xref> for a description of Boolean objects.
| 1. If _arg_ is a Number, return a new Number object whose [[NumberData]] internal slot is set to _arg_. See <emu-xref href="#sec-number-objects"></emu-xref> for a description of Number objects.
| 1. If _arg_ is a String, return a new String object whose [[StringData]] internal slot is set to _arg_. See <emu-xref href="#sec-string-objects"></emu-xref> for a description of String objects.
| 1. If _arg_ is a Symbol, return a new Symbol object whose [[SymbolData]] internal slot is set to _arg_. See <emu-xref href="#sec-symbol-objects"></emu-xref> for a description of Symbol objects.
| 1. If _arg_ is a BigInt, return a new BigInt object whose [[BigIntData]] internal slot is set to _arg_. See <emu-xref href="#sec-bigint-objects"></emu-xref> for a description of BigInt objects.
@@end

@@patch alg @sec-uint8array.prototype.tohex root:4..5

|   for_each byte : byte in toEncode {
|     let #"hex" = @sec-numeric-types-number-tostring($𝔽(byte), 16)
|     set var #"hex" = @sec-stringpad(#"hex", 2, "0", ~start~)
|     set var out = $"string-concatenation"(out, #"hex")
|   }
@@html

|   1. Set _out_ to the string-concatenation of _out_ and _hex_.

@@patch alg @sec-utf16encodecodepoint root:0..4
@@spectec
|   assert expr $Chain(0, data choice 1, ?codePoint, data choice 1, math(hex(1114111, "10FFFF")))
|   if codePoint <= math(hex(65535, "FFFF")) -> return $StrOfUnits($UnitOrPointOf(data choice 1, ?codePoint))
|   let cu1 = $UnitOrPointOf(data choice 1, $floor((codePoint - math(hex(65536, "10000"))) / math(hex(1024, "400"))) + math(hex(55296, "D800")))
|   let cu2 = $UnitOrPointOf(data choice 1, ((codePoint - math(hex(65536, "10000"))) modulo math(hex(1024, "400"))) + math(hex(56320, "DC00")))
@@html
| 1. Assert: 0 ≤ _codePoint_ ≤ 0x10FFFF.
| 1. If _codePoint_ ≤ 0xFFFF, return the String value consisting of the code unit whose numeric value is _codePoint_.
| 1. Let _cu1_ be the code unit whose numeric value is floor((_codePoint_ - 0x10000) / 0x400) + 0xD800.
| 1. Let _cu2_ be the code unit whose numeric value is ((_codePoint_ - 0x10000) modulo 0x400) + 0xDC00.
@@end

@@patch alg @sec-valid-chosen-reads root:0..1
@@spectec
|   for_each readEvent : ReadSharedMemory | ReadModifyWriteSharedMemory in @sec-sharedatablockeventset(execution) {
|     let chosenValueRecord = @ph-elem-whose(execution[[ChosenValues]], [[Event]], data choice 0, ?readEvent)
|     let chosenValue = chosenValueRecord[[ChosenValue]]
|     let readValue = @sec-valueofreadevent(execution, readEvent)
|     let chosenLength = $ListLen(?chosenValue)
|     let readLength = $ListLen(?readValue)
|     if chosenLength != readLength {
|       return false
|     }
|     let someDiffers = false
|     for_range i incl 0 .. excl chosenLength {
|       if chosenValue[i] != readValue[i] -> set var someDiffers = true
|     }
|     if someDiffers is val {true} {
|       return false
|     }
|   }
@@html
| 1. For each ReadSharedMemory or ReadModifyWriteSharedMemory event _readEvent_ of SharedDataBlockEventSet(_execution_), do
|   1. Let _chosenValueRecord_ be the element of _execution_.[[ChosenValues]] whose [[Event]] field is _readEvent_.
|   1. Let _chosenValue_ be _chosenValueRecord_.[[ChosenValue]].
|   1. Let _readValue_ be ValueOfReadEvent(_execution_, _readEvent_).
|   1. Let _chosenLength_ be the number of elements in _chosenValue_.
|   1. Let _readLength_ be the number of elements in _readValue_.
|   1. If _chosenLength_ ≠ _readLength_, then
|     1. Return *false*.
|   1. If _chosenValue_[_i_] ≠ _readValue_[_i_] for some integer _i_ in the interval from 0 (inclusive) to _chosenLength_ (exclusive), then
|     1. Return *false*.
@@end

@@patch alg @sec-wordcharacters root:1..4
@@spectec
|   if_else regexpRecord[[IgnoreCase]] is val {true} { let extraWordChars = $CharSetCanonExtra(basicWordChars, @sec-runtime-semantics-haseitherunicodeflag-abstract-operation(regexpRecord)) } else { let extraWordChars = $EmptyCharSet() }

|   }

| 1. Return the union of _basicWordChars_ and _extraWordChars_.
@@end

@@patch row @sec-assignment-operators-runtime-semantics-evaluation root:0..1
@@spectec
|     if child 5 |LeftHandSideExpression| is neither ty {|ObjectLiteral|; |ArrayLiteral|} {
|       let leftRef = ? sdo Evaluation(child 5 |LeftHandSideExpression|)
|       if sdo AssignmentTargetType(child 5 |LeftHandSideExpression|) is val {~web-compat~} -> throw @ph-new-error([[ReferenceError]])
|       if @sec-isanonymousfunctiondefinition(child 5 |AssignmentExpression|) is val {true} and sdo IsIdentifierRef(child 5 |LeftHandSideExpression|) is val {true} {
|         let lhs = sdo StringValue(child 5 |LeftHandSideExpression|)
|         let rightValue = ? sdo NamedEvaluation(child 5 |AssignmentExpression|; lhs)
|       }
|       else {
|         let rightRef = ? sdo Evaluation(child 5 |AssignmentExpression|)
|         let rightValue = ? @sec-getvalue(rightRef)
|       }
|       perform ? @sec-putvalue(leftRef, rightValue)
|       return rightValue
|     }
@@html
| 1. If |LeftHandSideExpression| is neither an |ObjectLiteral| nor an |ArrayLiteral|, then
|   1. Let _leftRef_ be ? Evaluation of |LeftHandSideExpression|.
|   1. If the AssignmentTargetType of |LeftHandSideExpression| is ~web-compat~, throw a *ReferenceError* exception.
|   1. If IsAnonymousFunctionDefinition(|AssignmentExpression|) is *true* and IsIdentifierRef of |LeftHandSideExpression| is *true*, then
|     1. Let _lhs_ be the StringValue of |LeftHandSideExpression|.
|     1. Let _rightValue_ be ? NamedEvaluation of |AssignmentExpression| with argument _lhs_.
|   1. Else,
|     1. Let _rightRef_ be ? Evaluation of |AssignmentExpression|.
|     1. Let _rightValue_ be ? GetValue(_rightRef_).
|   1. [id="step-assignmentexpression-evaluation-simple-putvalue"] Perform ? PutValue(_leftRef_, _rightValue_).
|   1. Return _rightValue_.

@@patch row @sec-function-calls-runtime-semantics-evaluation root:5..6
@@spectec
|     if #"ref" is ty {#"Reference Record"} and @sec-ispropertyreference(#"ref") is val {false} and #"ref"[[ReferencedName]] is val {"eval"} {
|       if @sec-samevalue(func, intrinsic [[eval]]) is val {true} {
|         let argList = ? sdo ArgumentListEvaluation(args)
|         if argList sat @ph-has-no-elements() -> return undefined
|         let evalArg = $ListElem(data choice 1, ?argList)
|         if_else @sec-isstrict(call_at @ph-this-nt(|CallExpression|)) is val {true} { let strictCaller = true } else { let strictCaller = false }
|         return ? @sec-performeval(evalArg, strictCaller, ~direct-eval~)
|       }
|     }
@@html
| 1. If _ref_ is a Reference Record, IsPropertyReference(_ref_) is *false*, and _ref_.[[ReferencedName]] is *"eval"*, then
|   1. If SameValue(_func_, %eval%) is *true*, then
|     1. Let _argList_ be ? ArgumentListEvaluation of _args_.
|     1. If _argList_ has no elements, return *undefined*.
|     1. Let _evalArg_ be the first element of _argList_.
|     1. If IsStrict(this |CallExpression|) is *true*, let _strictCaller_ be *true*; else let _strictCaller_ be *false*.
|     1. [id="step-callexpression-evaluation-direct-eval"] Return ? PerformEval(_evalArg_, _strictCaller_, ~direct-eval~).

@@spectec
| let values = @ph-contains-such-binds(data choice 0, data sort PrivateElement, ?existingElement, closure 2, container)
| if $ListLen(values) == 1 or $ListLen(values) != 0 and expr $""() {
|           let existingElement = values[0]
|           assert expr @ph-both-are(element[[Kind]], existingElement[[Kind]], ~accessor~)
|           if element[[Getter]] is val {undefined} {
|             let combined = record PrivateElement { [[Key]]: element[[Key]], [[Kind]]: ~accessor~, [[Getter]]: existingElement[[Getter]], [[Setter]]: element[[Setter]] }
|           }

|             let combined = record PrivateElement { [[Key]]: element[[Key]], [[Kind]]: ~accessor~, [[Getter]]: element[[Getter]], [[Setter]]: existingElement[[Setter]] }

|           perform @ph-replace-in(?existingElement, ?container, ?combined)
|         }
|         else {
|           perform $Append(?element, ?container)
|         }

|       1. Assert: _element_.[[Kind]] and _existingElement_.[[Kind]] are both ~accessor~.
|       1. If _element_.[[Getter]] is *undefined*, then
|         1. Let _combined_ be PrivateElement { [[Key]]: _element_.[[Key]], [[Kind]]: ~accessor~, [[Getter]]: _existingElement_.[[Getter]], [[Setter]]: _element_.[[Setter]] }.
|       1. Else,
|         1. Let _combined_ be PrivateElement { [[Key]]: _element_.[[Key]], [[Kind]]: ~accessor~, [[Getter]]: _element_.[[Getter]], [[Setter]]: _existingElement_.[[Setter]] }.
|       1. Replace _existingElement_ in _container_ with _combined_.

@@patch row @sec-withclausetoattributes#1 root:0..5
@@spectec
|     let attrs = sdo WithClauseToAttributes(child 5 |WithEntries|)
|     let #"rest" = $Copy(?attrs)
|     let sorted = $ListNew()
|     repeat_while #"rest" fails $IsEmpty() {
|       let least = $ListElem(data choice 1, ?#"rest")
|       for_each a in #"rest" {
|         if ! @sec-islessthan(a[[Key]], least[[Key]], true) is val {true} -> set var least = a
|       }
|       perform $Append(?least, ?sorted)
|       perform $RemoveElem(?least, ?#"rest")
|     }
|     set var attrs = sorted
@@html
| 1. Let _attrs_ be WithClauseToAttributes of |WithEntries|.
| 1. Sort _attrs_ according to the lexicographic order of their [[Key]] field, treating the value of each such field as a sequence of UTF-16 code unit values. NOTE: This sorting is observable only in that hosts are prohibited from changing behaviour based on the order in which attributes are enumerated.
@@end

@@patch tmpl @sec-nativeerror @sec-nativeerror/EvalError
@@spectec
|   if_else new_target is val {undefined} { let newTarget = $ActiveFunction() } else { let newTarget = new_target }
|   let #"obj" = ? @sec-ordinarycreatefromconstructor(newTarget, "%EvalError.prototype%", [[[ErrorData]]])
|   if message is not val {undefined} {
|     let messageString = ? @sec-tostring(message)
|     perform @sec-createnonenumerabledatapropertyorthrow(#"obj", "message", messageString)
|   }
|   perform ? @sec-installerrorcause(#"obj", options)
|   perform $HostExtendObject(#"obj")
|   return #"obj"
@@html
| 
|             1. If NewTarget is *undefined*, let _newTarget_ be the active function object; else let _newTarget_ be NewTarget.
|             1. [id="step-nativeerror-ordinarycreatefromconstructor"] Let _obj_ be ? OrdinaryCreateFromConstructor(_newTarget_, *"%<var>NativeError</var>.prototype%"*, « [[ErrorData]] »).
|             1. If _message_ is not *undefined*, then
|               1. Let _messageString_ be ? ToString(_message_).
|               1. Perform CreateNonEnumerableDataPropertyOrThrow(_obj_, *"message"*, _messageString_).
|             1. Perform ? InstallErrorCause(_obj_, _options_).
|             1. Return _obj_.
|           
@@end

@@patch tmpl @sec-typedarray @sec-typedarray/BigInt64Array
@@spectec
|   if new_target is val {undefined} -> throw @ph-new-error([[TypeError]])
|   let ctorName = @ph-typedarray-constructor-name()
|   let proto = "%BigInt64Array.prototype%"
|   let numberOfArgs = $ListLen(?args)
|   if numberOfArgs == 0 -> return ? @sec-allocatetypedarray(ctorName, new_target, proto, 0)
|   let firstArg = args[0]
|   if firstArg is ty {Object} {
|     let #"obj" = ? @sec-allocatetypedarray(ctorName, new_target, proto)
|     if firstArg sat $HasSlot([[TypedArrayName]]) {
|       perform ? @sec-initializetypedarrayfromtypedarray(#"obj", firstArg)
|     }
|     else_if firstArg sat $HasSlot([[ArrayBufferData]]) {
|       if_else numberOfArgs > 1 { let byteOffset = args[1] } else { let byteOffset = undefined }
|       if_else numberOfArgs > 2 { let length = args[2] } else { let length = undefined }
|       perform ? @sec-initializetypedarrayfromarraybuffer(#"obj", firstArg, byteOffset, length)
|     }
|     else {
|       assert firstArg is ty {Object}
|       assert firstArg fails $HasSlot([[TypedArrayName]])
|       assert firstArg fails $HasSlot([[ArrayBufferData]])
|       perform ? @sec-initializetypedarrayfromelements(#"obj", firstArg)
|     }
|     return #"obj"
|   }
|   assert firstArg is not ty {Object}
|   let elementLength = ? @sec-toindex(firstArg)
|   return ? @sec-allocatetypedarray(ctorName, new_target, proto, elementLength)
@@html
| 
|           1. If NewTarget is *undefined*, throw a *TypeError* exception.
|           1. Let _ctorName_ be the String value of the Constructor Name value specified in <emu-xref href="#table-the-typedarray-constructors"></emu-xref> for this <var>TypedArray</var> constructor.
|           1. Let _proto_ be <code>"%<var>TypedArray</var>.prototype%"</code>.
|           1. Let _numberOfArgs_ be the number of elements in _args_.
|           1. If _numberOfArgs_ = 0, return ? AllocateTypedArray(_ctorName_, NewTarget, _proto_, 0).
|           1. Let _firstArg_ be _args_[0].
|           1. If _firstArg_ is an Object, then
|             1. Let _obj_ be ? AllocateTypedArray(_ctorName_, NewTarget, _proto_).
|             1. If _firstArg_ has a [[TypedArrayName]] internal slot, then
|               1. Perform ? InitializeTypedArrayFromTypedArray(_obj_, _firstArg_).
|             1. Else if _firstArg_ has an [[ArrayBufferData]] internal slot, then
|               1. If _numberOfArgs_ > 1, let _byteOffset_ be _args_[1]; else let _byteOffset_ be *undefined*.
|               1. If _numberOfArgs_ > 2, let _length_ be _args_[2]; else let _length_ be *undefined*.
|               1. Perform ? InitializeTypedArrayFromArrayBuffer(_obj_, _firstArg_, _byteOffset_, _length_).
|             1. Else,
|               1. Assert: _firstArg_ is an Object and _firstArg_ does not have either a [[TypedArrayName]] or an [[ArrayBufferData]] internal slot.
|               1. Perform ? InitializeTypedArrayFromElements(_obj_, _firstArg_).
|             1. Return _obj_.
|           1. Assert: _firstArg_ is not an Object.
|           1. Let _elementLength_ be ? ToIndex(_firstArg_).
|           1. Return ? AllocateTypedArray(_ctorName_, NewTarget, _proto_, _elementLength_).
|         
@@end
